</p>
<div class="quote">
<dl><dd><span title="Source: The Hunger Games: Mockingjay - Part 2">"<i>Katniss Everdeen; a small town girl from <a href="/wiki/District_12" title="District 12">District 12</a> who survived the <a href="/wiki/Hunger_Games_(event)" title="Hunger Games (event)">Hunger Games</a> and turned a nation of slaves into an army!</i>"</span></dd>
<dd>—President <a href="/wiki/Alma_Coin" title="Alma Coin">Alma Coin</a> describing Katniss<sup class="noprint"><a href="/wiki/The_Hunger_Games:_Mockingjay_-_Part_2" title="The Hunger Games: Mockingjay - Part 2">[src]</a></sup></dd></dl></div>
<p><b>Katniss Everdeen</b> is the main protagonist and narrator of <a href="/wiki/The_Hunger_Games_trilogy" title="The Hunger Games trilogy"><i>The Hunger Games</i> trilogy</a>. After her younger sister, <a href="/wiki/Primrose_Everdeen" title="Primrose Everdeen">Primrose Everdeen</a>, was <a href="/wiki/Reaping" title="Reaping">reaped</a> in the <a href="/wiki/74th_Hunger_Games" title="74th Hunger Games">74th Hunger Games</a>, Katniss <a href="/wiki/Volunteer" title="Volunteer">volunteered</a> to take her place as the female <a href="/wiki/Tribute" title="Tribute">tribute</a> from <a href="/wiki/District_12" title="District 12">District 12</a>. This action set in motion the events of the entire series. She is the female <a href="/wiki/Victor" title="Victor">victor</a> from <a href="/wiki/District_12" title="District 12">District 12</a> for the 74th Hunger Games, and the female <a href="/wiki/Tribute" title="Tribute">tribute</a> from <a href="/wiki/District_12" title="District 12">District 12</a> for the <a href="/wiki/75th_Hunger_Games" title="75th Hunger Games">75th Hunger Games</a>. Katniss is the oldest daughter of <a href="/wiki/Asterid_Everdeen" title="Asterid Everdeen">Asterid</a> and <a href="/wiki/Burdock_Everdeen" title="Burdock Everdeen">Burdock Everdeen</a>. 
</p>

			<h3 class="trfc161__header-text">Quick Answers</h3>
		</div>
		<img class="trfc161-logo" src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/8941553e-df6a-4310-a080-b9da56fdcbdd" alt="fandom logo">
	</div>
	<div class="trfc161__groups">
			<details class="trfc161__group dark-theme" data-question-id="1142" data-revision-id="2">
				<summary class="trfc161__question-wrapper">
					<b class="trfc161__question">Who is Katniss Everdeen?</b>
						<img class="trfc161__question-chevron" src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/36002e46-06fe-4633-9d99-27d96cf5bcdc" alt="toggle section">
				</summary>
				<div class="trfc161__answer">
					<div class="quick-answers__answer-text">
						Katniss Everdeen is the main protagonist and narrator of The Hunger Games trilogy. She was born on May 8th in District 12, and when she was eleven, she lost her father in a mine explosion. She stepped up as the head of her household, hunting to provide for her younger sister Prim and their mother. When Katniss was sixteen, Prim was reaped to participate in the 74th Hunger Games, and Katniss volunteered to take her place. This action set the events of the entire series into motion.
					</div>
					<div class="trfc161__answer-source">
						Provided by: Community
					</div>
					<div class="quick_answers__report-module-root" data-reportable="1"></div>
				</div>
			</details>
			<details class="trfc161__group dark-theme" data-question-id="1143" data-revision-id="4">
				<summary class="trfc161__question-wrapper">
					<b class="trfc161__question">How old was Katniss when she gave birth?</b>
						<img class="trfc161__question-chevron" src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/36002e46-06fe-4633-9d99-27d96cf5bcdc" alt="toggle section">
				</summary>
				<div class="trfc161__answer">
					<div class="quick-answers__answer-text">
						Katniss was 17 throughout the events of Mockingjay, and she agreed to have children with Peeta Mellark fifteen years after the war, so she was 32-33 when she had her first child, a daughter. Her son was a toddler during the epilogue, narrated more than 20 years after the war, so she was most likely in her mid-to-late 30s when she had him.
					</div>
					<div class="trfc161__answer-source">
						Provided by: Community
					</div>
					<div class="quick_answers__report-module-root" data-reportable="1"></div>
				</div>
			</details>
			<details class="trfc161__group dark-theme" data-question-id="1145" data-revision-id="2">
				<summary class="trfc161__question-wrapper">
					<b class="trfc161__question">What kind of character is Katniss?</b>
						<img class="trfc161__question-chevron" src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/36002e46-06fe-4633-9d99-27d96cf5bcdc" alt="toggle section">
				</summary>
				<div class="trfc161__answer">
					<div class="quick-answers__answer-text">
						Katniss Everdeen is the main protagonist and narrator of The Hunger Games trilogy. She is a skilled archer, and she is strong-willed, independent, determined, compassionate, and highly goal-oriented.
					</div>
					<div class="trfc161__answer-source">
						Provided by: Community
					</div>
					<div class="quick_answers__report-module-root" data-reportable="1"></div>
				</div>
			</details>
			<details class="trfc161__group dark-theme" data-question-id="1146" data-revision-id="2">
				<summary class="trfc161__question-wrapper">
					<b class="trfc161__question">What happened during Katniss's pregnancy?</b>
						<img class="trfc161__question-chevron" src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/36002e46-06fe-4633-9d99-27d96cf5bcdc" alt="toggle section">
				</summary>
				<div class="trfc161__answer">
					<div class="quick-answers__answer-text">
						Around fifteen years after the end of the war, Katniss and Peeta had their first child together. In the epilogue, Katniss described experiencing a terrible fear at the feeling of her daughter moving inside her, and it took finally holding her daughter in her arms to get over this fear. Her pregnancy with her son was "a little easier, but not much."
					</div>
					<div class="trfc161__answer-source">
						Provided by: Community
					</div>
					<div class="quick_answers__report-module-root" data-reportable="1"></div>
				</div>
			</details>
	</div>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Biography">Biography</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D1&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Early_life">Early life</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D2&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>Katniss was born on May 8th in <a href="/wiki/District_12" title="District 12">District 12</a> to <a href="/wiki/Burdock_Everdeen" title="Burdock Everdeen">Burdock</a> and <a href="/wiki/Asterid_Everdeen" title="Asterid Everdeen">Asterid Everdeen</a> before the 58th Hunger Games. Her father was a <a href="/wiki/Coal_miner" title="Coal miner">miner</a><sup id="cite_ref-THG_22_10-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_22-10"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>10<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> and illegal hunter who traded in <a href="/wiki/The_Hob" title="The Hob">the Hob</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_2_11-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_2-11"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_4_12-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_4-12"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Her mother was a healer who used to work at her parents' apothecary. Four years after Katniss was born, they had a second daughter, Primrose.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_2_11-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_2-11"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>When Katniss was a child, her father would bring her out into the <a href="/wiki/Woods" title="Woods">woods</a> beyond District 12 to teach her how to hunt with a bow and arrow, as well as how to forage for <a href="/wiki/Katniss_(plant)" title="Katniss (plant)">edible plants</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_4_12-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_4-12"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Later on, this would be where Katniss and <a href="/wiki/Gale" class="mw-redirect" title="Gale">Gale</a> would hide out. Katniss's father would sometimes bring her to a <a href="/wiki/Lake" title="Lake">lake</a> far into the woods where she learned how to swim.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_3_13-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_3-13"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> It was during one of these lake visits that Mr. Everdeen introduced Katniss to the edible plant she was named after, and told her, "As long as you can find yourself, you'll never starve."<sup id="cite_ref-THG_4_12-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_4-12"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Katniss was close to her father and shared his appreciation for music. He was a good singer; he was so good that the mockingjays in the woods would stop to listen to him and copy his tunes.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_3_14-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_3-14"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> At some point, he taught her "<a href="/wiki/The_Hanging_Tree_(song)" title="The Hanging Tree (song)">The Hanging Tree</a>", a song about a man calling for his lover to meet him in death. When Katniss's mother heard her singing it, she began screaming at her father, telling him that it was unsuitable to be heard by small children. This was heightened when she saw Katniss and Prim make actual 'necklaces of rope', with the sisters being oblivious to the fact that the song was talking about voluntarily hanging themselves to death. She then banned the song from their home and forbade Katniss to sing it again outside. Although Katniss never sang it in her mother's presence again, she later sang it during the rebellion when she thought of her father.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_9_15-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_9-15"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>When Katniss was eleven years old, her father died in a mine explosion<sup id="cite_ref-THG_1_2-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_1-2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, causing her mother to fall into a deep depression and sadness. She became still and weak, refusing to listen to her daughter's pleas and becoming jobless. Unable to stir her, Katniss became the head and primary breadwinner of the family. In order to be resourceful, Katniss made their rations last as long as possible. When they ran out, she attempted to sell some old clothes to make some money, to no avail. She tried to look in the garbage bins of the houses in the town square but was yelled at by the <a href="/wiki/Mrs._Mellark" title="Mrs. Mellark">baker's wife</a> for snooping. <a href="/wiki/Peeta_Mellark" title="Peeta Mellark">Peeta Mellark</a>, the baker's son, gave Katniss some burnt bread that he'd been told to give to the pig. It is implied that Peeta burnt the bread specifically so he could give it to Katniss and her family to save their lives.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_2_11-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_2-11"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Katniss began to remember the knowledge her father taught her about the woods and about feeding herself. As she gained the courage to move deeper into the forest, she found more game and was better able to feed her family. Katniss's mother began to return from her depression, but Katniss could no longer trust her or rely on her.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_4_12-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_4-12"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> When she turned twelve, Katniss signed up for <a href="/wiki/Tesserae" class="mw-redirect" title="Tesserae">tesserae</a>, causing her name to be entered four times in her first reaping. Despite this, Katniss was not chosen as tribute.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_1_2-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_1-2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<figure class="thumb mw-halign-left show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 180px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/8/83/THG_IE_Gale.png/revision/latest?cb=20250315222919" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss first meeting Gale in " src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/8/83/THG_IE_Gale.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20250315222919" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="180" height="225" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="THG IE Gale.png" data-image-key="THG_IE_Gale.png" data-relevant="1"></a> 	 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:THG_IE_Gale.png" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Katniss first meeting Gale in <i><a href="/wiki/The_Hunger_Games:_Illustrated_Edition" title="The Hunger Games: Illustrated Edition">The Hunger Games: Illustrated Edition</a></i></p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<p>In the woods, twelve-year-old Katniss met fourteen-year-old <a href="/wiki/Gale_Hawthorne" title="Gale Hawthorne">Gale Hawthorne</a>, an older boy whose father died in the same mining explosion that killed her father. Gale provided for his family by setting traps and snares to catch game. The two eventually became a team, and Katniss provided Gale with one of the bows her father made; she taught him how to shoot and he taught her about snares.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_8_16-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_8-16"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> The two agreed to protect each other's families if one of them were to be reaped.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_3_14-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_3-14"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Katniss, like all children in Panem, was required to attend school (although she was largely uninterested because she didn't see how it would bring her food on the table<sup id="cite_ref-THG_3_14-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_3-14"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>) except on certain holidays, including the reaping. Unsure of her social skills and uninterested in making friends, Katniss's only school friend was <a href="/wiki/Madge_Undersee" title="Madge Undersee">Madge Undersee</a>, though Katniss felt they were more like acquaintances than friends, as they rarely spoke and mostly just sat together at mealtimes and in classes.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_1_2-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_1-2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="74th_Hunger_Games">74th Hunger Games</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D3&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div class="quote">
<dl><dd><span title="Source: The Hunger Games">"<i>I volunteer! I volunteer as tribute!</i>"</span></dd>
<dd>—Katniss at the Reaping<sup class="noprint"><a href="/wiki/The_Hunger_Games" title="The Hunger Games">[src]</a></sup></dd></dl></div><figure class="thumb  show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 160px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/8/8e/Katniss_%282%29.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20121021224831" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss during The Hunger Games." src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="160" height="268" class="thumbimage lazyload" data-image-name="Katniss (2).jpg" data-image-key="Katniss_%282%29.jpg" data-relevant="0" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/8/8e/Katniss_%282%29.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20121021224831"></a> 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/8/8e/Katniss_%282%29.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20121021224831" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss during The Hunger Games." src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/8/8e/Katniss_%282%29.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20121021224831" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="160" height="268" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="Katniss (2).jpg" data-image-key="Katniss_%282%29.jpg" data-relevant="0" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/8/8e/Katniss_%282%29.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20121021224831"></a></noscript> 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:Katniss_(2).jpg" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Katniss during <i>The Hunger Games</i>.</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="The_Reaping">The Reaping</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D4&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>When she was 16 years old, Katniss volunteered to take the place of her sister<sup id="cite_ref-THG_2_11-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_2-11"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, <a href="/wiki/Primrose_Everdeen" title="Primrose Everdeen">Primrose Everdeen</a>, in the <a href="/wiki/74th_Hunger_Games" title="74th Hunger Games">74th Hunger Games</a> after Prim's name was called during the reaping. Originally, she had no intention of going into the Games, hoping she wouldn't be picked before the female tribute was chosen.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_1_2-7" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_1-2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> It had not even crossed her mind that her sister would be picked, but when Prim was reaped, Katniss volunteered to save her, despite knowing she would likely die in her sister's place. The male tribute was Peeta Mellark.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_2_11-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_2-11"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Before she leaves for <a href="/wiki/The_Capitol" title="The Capitol">the Capitol</a>, Katniss goes to the <a href="/wiki/Justice_Building" title="Justice Building">Justice Building</a> to say her final goodbyes to her mother, <a href="/wiki/Gale_Hawthorne" title="Gale Hawthorne">Gale</a>, Prim, <a href="/wiki/Madge_Undersee" title="Madge Undersee">Madge Undersee</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Mr._Mellark" class="mw-redirect" title="Mr. Mellark">Mr. Mellark</a>. When she says goodbye to her mother and Prim, Prim makes her promise to try to win, so Katniss complies and tells her mother to make a promise that she won't leave Prim alone to take care of herself. Her mother tries to explain to Katniss that she was ill, but Katniss still makes her promise to take care of Prim, and she finally complies. <a href="/wiki/Madge_Undersee" title="Madge Undersee">Madge Undersee</a> comes in to say her final goodbye to her by giving her the <a href="/wiki/Mockingjay_pin" title="Mockingjay pin">mockingjay pin</a> to use as her <a href="/wiki/Tribute_token" title="Tribute token">tribute token</a>. The baker, Peeta's father, comes in and gives her cookies, a luxury, and tells her he will not let her family starve. Gale tries to give her a final piece of advice but is dragged out before he can finish, leaving Katniss to wonder about what Gale wanted to say so badly. The district escort, <a href="/wiki/Effie_Trinket" title="Effie Trinket">Effie Trinket</a>, then takes Peeta and Katniss aboard the train to <a href="/wiki/The_Capitol" title="The Capitol">the Capitol</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_3_14-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_3-14"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> On their way, Katniss and Peeta meet their drunken mentor, <a href="/wiki/Haymitch_Abernathy" title="Haymitch Abernathy">Haymitch Abernathy</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_4_12-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_4-12"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>In the film, Katniss had gone to visit <a href="/wiki/The_Hob" title="The Hob">the Hob</a> before the reaping, and <a href="/wiki/Greasy_Sae" title="Greasy Sae">Greasy Sae</a> gave her the mockingjay pin, which Katniss then entrusted to Prim to help her feel safer. During their final goodbyes, Prim gives the pin back to Katniss.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_17-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG-17"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>17<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="The_Parade">The Parade</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D5&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<figure class="thumb mw-halign-left show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 180px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/9/90/Katniss%E2%80%99s_outfit_for_the_parade.webp/revision/latest?cb=20221227201109" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss in her outfit for the parade" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="180" height="238" class="thumbimage lazyload" data-image-name="Katniss’s outfit for the parade.webp" data-image-key="Katniss%E2%80%99s_outfit_for_the_parade.webp" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/9/90/Katniss%E2%80%99s_outfit_for_the_parade.webp/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20221227201109"></a> 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/9/90/Katniss%E2%80%99s_outfit_for_the_parade.webp/revision/latest?cb=20221227201109" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss in her outfit for the parade" src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/9/90/Katniss%E2%80%99s_outfit_for_the_parade.webp/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20221227201109" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="180" height="238" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="Katniss’s outfit for the parade.webp" data-image-key="Katniss%E2%80%99s_outfit_for_the_parade.webp" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/9/90/Katniss%E2%80%99s_outfit_for_the_parade.webp/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20221227201109"></a></noscript> 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:Katniss%E2%80%99s_outfit_for_the_parade.webp" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Katniss in her outfit for the parade</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<p>When Katniss arrives at the Capitol, she is fussed over by her <a href="/wiki/Prep_team" title="Prep team">prep team</a>, which consists of <a href="/wiki/Octavia" title="Octavia">Octavia</a>, <a href="/wiki/Flavius" title="Flavius">Flavius</a> and <a href="/wiki/Venia" title="Venia">Venia</a>. She then meets her stylist, <a href="/wiki/Cinna" title="Cinna">Cinna</a>, who comes up with an idea to make Katniss and Peeta unforgettable during the parade. Cinna dresses her up in a black unitard to represent coal and lights her and Peeta with synthetic <a href="/wiki/Fire" title="Fire">fire</a> that does not burn them.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_5_18-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_5-18"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>18<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> After the parade, they are escorted to their residence. At dinner time, Katniss meets a haunting face from the past, <a href="/wiki/Lavinia" title="Lavinia">a girl</a> she didn't get to save, who has now become an <a href="/wiki/Avox" title="Avox">Avox</a>. When she says that she recognizes the girl, Peeta lies to help cover for her; he suggests that the Avox resembles <a href="/wiki/Delly_Cartwright" title="Delly Cartwright">Delly Cartwright</a>, although they actually look very different.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_6_19-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_6-19"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>19<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Training_Sessions">Training Sessions</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D6&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>The next morning, Katniss goes down to the <a href="/wiki/Training_Center" title="Training Center">Training Center</a> to prepare for the Games. Haymitch advises her not to show her all of her skills until the private sessions with the <a href="/wiki/Gamemaker" title="Gamemaker">Gamemakers</a>. During training, Katniss and Peeta notice they are being followed by a little girl named <a href="/wiki/Rue" title="Rue">Rue</a>, from <a href="/wiki/District_11" title="District 11">District 11</a>.
</p><p>During the private session, Katniss shows the Gamemakers her skills with a bow and arrow. Katniss first shoots at the practice dummy but misses, unaccustomed to the bow she is using. The Gamemakers start ignoring her and pay more attention to a roast pig since she is from <a href="/wiki/District_12" title="District 12">District 12</a>, a district that is not known for producing many <a href="/wiki/Victors" class="mw-redirect" title="Victors">victors</a>. Along with that, she was the last person who got to show their skills. Out of frustration, Katniss targets the apple in the roasted pig's mouth, successfully hitting it, causing reactions of shock and amazement among the <a href="/wiki/Gamemakers" class="mw-redirect" title="Gamemakers">Gamemakers</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_7_20-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_7-20"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>20<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Katniss is, at first, outraged at her own behavior, later thinking she shouldn't have shot the pig, crying in her bed. However, Katniss scores an 11 for her fiery temper and attitude, and is surprised later on.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_8_16-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_8-16"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Interview">Interview</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D7&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>While preparing for <a href="/wiki/Interviews" title="Interviews">interview</a> night, Katniss is stressed out by <a href="/wiki/Haymitch" class="mw-redirect" title="Haymitch">Haymitch</a> and <a href="/wiki/Effie" class="mw-redirect" title="Effie">Effie</a> trying to prepare her. <a href="/wiki/Cinna" title="Cinna">Cinna</a> dresses her in a dress covered in jewels that make her look like she is being engulfed by flames whenever she moves. He gives her advice before she does her interview, being the only one she truly likes and respects. She is interviewed by <a href="/wiki/Caesar_Flickerman" title="Caesar Flickerman">Caesar Flickerman</a> and she talks about her stay in the Capitol. She becomes something of a celebrity in <a href="/wiki/Panem" title="Panem">Panem</a> and is given the advantage of sympathy after <a href="/wiki/Peeta" class="mw-redirect" title="Peeta">Peeta</a> admits to all of Panem that he loves her, making Katniss blush at first. In private, she becomes furious and shoves Peeta before calming down and apologizing.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_9_21-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_9-21"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>21<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_10_22-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_10-22"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="The_Arena">The Arena</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D8&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<figure class="thumb  show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 200px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/3/34/Caesar_and_Katniss.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20120210005640" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss with ." src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="200" height="270" class="thumbimage lazyload" data-image-name="Caesar and Katniss.jpg" data-image-key="Caesar_and_Katniss.jpg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/3/34/Caesar_and_Katniss.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/200?cb=20120210005640"></a> 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/3/34/Caesar_and_Katniss.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20120210005640" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss with ." src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/3/34/Caesar_and_Katniss.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/200?cb=20120210005640" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="200" height="270" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="Caesar and Katniss.jpg" data-image-key="Caesar_and_Katniss.jpg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/3/34/Caesar_and_Katniss.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/200?cb=20120210005640"></a></noscript> 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:Caesar_and_Katniss.jpg" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Katniss with <a href="/wiki/Caesar_Flickerman" title="Caesar Flickerman">Caesar Flickerman</a>.</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<p>The night before the Games, she is terrified of the horrors that await her in the arena. She goes to the rooftop and talks with Peeta. Peeta tells her that he wants to die as himself and show that he is more than just a piece in the Capitol's <a href="/wiki/The_Hunger_Games_(event)" class="mw-redirect" title="The Hunger Games (event)">Games</a>. Katniss tells him that she can't afford to think like that and leaves when he doubts he will win.
</p><p>On the day of the Games, Cinna dresses Katniss in her uniform for the games. While waiting to launch, Katniss and Cinna talk to each other. He tells Katniss that he can't bet on any tributes due to his part in the preparation of the Games, but if he could, he would bet on her. Katniss finds some confidence in this and the speaker announces the tributes to launch.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_10_22-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_10-22"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Cornucopia_Bloodbath">Cornucopia Bloodbath</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D9&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>When the Games begin, Katniss gets away from the <a href="/wiki/Cornucopia" title="Cornucopia">Cornucopia</a> quickly, trying to follow the advice of her mentor, but gets into a minor tussle with the <a href="/wiki/District_9_male_(74th_HG)" title="District 9 male (74th HG)">District 9 male</a> over an orange backpack which ends when the boy is cleaved in the back by <a href="/wiki/Clove" title="Clove">Clove</a>, the girl from <a href="/wiki/District_2" title="District 2">District 2</a>. The backpack contains a sleeping bag, a bottle of iodine (a chemical that purifies water) along with a dropper, a water bottle, crackers, dried beef strips, wire, and a pair of "sunglasses"<sup id="cite_ref-THG_11_23-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_11-23"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>23<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> that turn out to be night vision glasses.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_15_24-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_15-24"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>24<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>.
</p><p>Katniss is almost killed in the bloodbath when <a href="/wiki/Clove" title="Clove">Clove</a> aims a knife at her head. She blocks it with her backpack and earns a knife as a result. Clove does not pursue her, and she heads back to the Cornucopia before all the good stuff is taken.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_11_23-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_11-23"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>23<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Katniss runs into <a href="/wiki/Foxface" class="mw-redirect" title="Foxface">Foxface</a> in the forest and they part ways in a panic. She also has a brief encounter with the <a href="/wiki/District_10_male_(74th_HG)" class="mw-redirect" title="District 10 male (74th HG)">District 10 male</a>, but runs in the opposite direction, and he does not pursue her.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_17-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG-17"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>17<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> The first night, she climbs a tree to sleep in. In the middle of the night, she is woken up by the <a href="/wiki/Heddle" title="Heddle">District 8 female</a>, who Katniss describes as "the biggest idiot in the Games" for starting a fire that would attract obvious attention. Shortly after this the Career tributes and Peeta attack the girl, nearly killing her. The Careers argue over whether or not the girl is dead, which ends in Peeta saying he will go back to finish her off. When Katniss hears this, she is shocked that Peeta has teamed up with the Careers.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_11_23-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_11-23"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>23<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Over the next few days, Katniss moves farther away from the Cornucopia, though she is slowed down by dehydration. She nearly dies of thirst before she finds a small pond to drink from, stumbling onto it while she camouflages her bright orange backpack with mud so that it wouldn't draw too much attention.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_12_25-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_12-25"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Being_Hunted_by_the_Career_Tributes">Being Hunted by the Career Tributes</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D10&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>The next morning, she is awoken by a forest fire<sup id="cite_ref-THG_12_25-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_12-25"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> created by the Gamemakers, including fireballs that are flung at her. She survives the attack but suffers minor burns and cuts on her hands and a severe burn on her calf. She goes to sleep and is woken by the sound of footsteps. Katniss is briefly chased by the <a href="/wiki/Career_Tribute" title="Career Tribute">Career Tributes</a> and Peeta, but she escapes up a tree. Cato tries to climb up after Katniss, but crashes to the ground because he is too heavy for the branches to hold. Glimmer also attempts to kill Katniss by shooting her with an arrow, but she misses. The Careers realize that they are all too heavy to climb the tree, so they decide to wait for her to come down.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_13_26-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_13-26"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>26<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Alliance_with_Rue">Alliance with Rue</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D11&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>Suddenly, <a href="/wiki/Rue" title="Rue">Rue</a> appears high up in a neighboring tree and points to the <a href="/wiki/Tracker_jacker" title="Tracker jacker">tracker jacker</a> nest above Katniss, showing her what to do with it.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_13_26-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_13-26"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>26<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>The next morning, Katniss warns <a href="/wiki/Rue" title="Rue">Rue</a> she is about to cut the nest down. Katniss cuts it down with the knife she got from <a href="/wiki/Clove" title="Clove">Clove</a> and the tracker jackers attack the Careers, killing <a href="/wiki/Glimmer" title="Glimmer">Glimmer</a> and the girl tribute from <a href="/wiki/District_4" title="District 4">District 4</a>. The Careers run to the lake after being stung by the tracker jackers. Katniss is also stung and starts to run in the other direction. Katniss remembers that <a href="/wiki/Glimmer" title="Glimmer">Glimmer</a> had a silver bow when the Careers attacked her (the only bow in the entire arena<sup id="cite_ref-THG_11_23-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_11-23"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>23<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>), so she goes back for it. She retrieves it from Glimmer's bloated body, but as she's about to run away, her hallucinations begin. A delusional Katniss is saved by Peeta when she's unable to coordinate herself to run from <a href="/wiki/Cato" title="Cato">Cato</a>, who is quickly approaching. After she runs away, she blacks out from the tracker jacker venom.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_14_27-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_14-27"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>27<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<figure class="thumb mw-halign-left show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 180px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/3/37/Katniss_and_RueStanding.png/revision/latest?cb=20131207074453" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss alliance with ." src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="180" height="216" class="thumbimage lazyload" data-image-name="Katniss and RueStanding.png" data-image-key="Katniss_and_RueStanding.png" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/3/37/Katniss_and_RueStanding.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20131207074453"></a> 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/3/37/Katniss_and_RueStanding.png/revision/latest?cb=20131207074453" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss alliance with ." src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/3/37/Katniss_and_RueStanding.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20131207074453" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="180" height="216" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="Katniss and RueStanding.png" data-image-key="Katniss_and_RueStanding.png" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/3/37/Katniss_and_RueStanding.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20131207074453"></a></noscript> 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:Katniss_and_RueStanding.png" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Katniss alliance with <a href="/wiki/Rue" title="Rue">Rue</a>.</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<p>When Katniss wakes up, she finds out that Rue is watching her. She then proposes an alliance.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_15_24-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_15-24"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>24<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> She says Rue reminds her of Prim, in both size and demeanor. She tries to give Rue her pin, but Rue says no, saying that that was how she came to trust Katniss in the first place, seeing the <a href="/wiki/Mockingjay_(bird)" title="Mockingjay (bird)">mockingjay</a> on her pin. Rue also explains that the mockingjay is used in the District 11 orchards to tell the workers when time is up and when they can go home.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_16_28-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_16-28"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>28<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Katniss and Rue then plan to destroy the Careers' food and supply pile,<sup id="cite_ref-THG_15_24-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_15-24"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>24<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> with Katniss figuring that the Careers have had plenty of food their whole lives and would struggle if a direct food supply was taken away from them. Rue provides a distraction while Katniss checks out the Career camp. By watching <a href="/wiki/Foxface" class="mw-redirect" title="Foxface">Foxface</a>, the <a href="/wiki/District_5" title="District 5">District 5</a> tribute, she discovers that the food is protected by landmines, and she shoots a sack that spills apples onto the ground, setting off all of the landmines.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_16_28-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_16-28"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>28<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Nearly everything is destroyed, but due to the explosion and her relative proximity, Katniss becomes deaf in her left ear,<sup id="cite_ref-THG_17_29-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_17-29"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>29<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> though this injury is later surgically repaired after the Games.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_26_30-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_26-30"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>30<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Death_of_Rue">Death of Rue</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D12&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4><p>
Katniss goes to the woods, listening for Rue. Her <a href="/wiki/Rue%27s_four-note_song" title="Rue's four-note song">four-note melody</a> rings out through the woods as the mockingjays adopt it, but she suddenly cries out for Katniss. Katniss runs through the trees to find Rue entangled in a mesh net, one arm sticking out before a spear is thrown into her abdomen by the boy from <a href="/wiki/District_1" title="District 1">District 1</a>,<sup id="cite_ref-THG_17_29-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_17-29"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>29<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> whose name is later revealed to be Marvel.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_5_31-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_5-31"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>31<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Katniss shoots an arrow at Marvel's neck, killing him. Katniss then goes over to comfort Rue, promising she'll win for the both of them, and sings the valley song, "Deep in the Meadow", that she sang to comfort Prim the morning of the reaping. </p><figure class="thumb  show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 289px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/0/00/2011-11-15-10-38-33-5-katniss-delivers-a-farewell-salute-this-gesture-i.jpeg/revision/latest?cb=20111123012923" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss giving the silent salute in the arena." src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="289" height="180" class="thumbimage lazyload" data-image-name="2011-11-15-10-38-33-5-katniss-delivers-a-farewell-salute-this-gesture-i.jpeg" data-image-key="2011-11-15-10-38-33-5-katniss-delivers-a-farewell-salute-this-gesture-i.jpeg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/0/00/2011-11-15-10-38-33-5-katniss-delivers-a-farewell-salute-this-gesture-i.jpeg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/289?cb=20111123012923"></a> 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/0/00/2011-11-15-10-38-33-5-katniss-delivers-a-farewell-salute-this-gesture-i.jpeg/revision/latest?cb=20111123012923" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss giving the silent salute in the arena." src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/0/00/2011-11-15-10-38-33-5-katniss-delivers-a-farewell-salute-this-gesture-i.jpeg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/289?cb=20111123012923" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="289" height="180" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="2011-11-15-10-38-33-5-katniss-delivers-a-farewell-salute-this-gesture-i.jpeg" data-image-key="2011-11-15-10-38-33-5-katniss-delivers-a-farewell-salute-this-gesture-i.jpeg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/0/00/2011-11-15-10-38-33-5-katniss-delivers-a-farewell-salute-this-gesture-i.jpeg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/289?cb=20111123012923"></a></noscript> 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:2011-11-15-10-38-33-5-katniss-delivers-a-farewell-salute-this-gesture-i.jpeg" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Katniss giving the silent salute in the arena.</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<p><a href="/wiki/Rue" title="Rue">Rue</a> dies after Katniss finishes the song. Angered at the Capitol's cruelty, Katniss picks white flowers and decorates Rue's body with them, hiding the wound and weaving them into her hair. When she finishes, Katniss realizes how peaceful Rue looks and does the signature <a href="/wiki/District_12" title="District 12">District 12</a> farewell gesture to Rue. Later, this is seen as an act of defiance to the Capitol.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_27_32-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_27-32"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>32<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> She then takes <a href="/wiki/Rue" title="Rue">Rue</a> and Marvel's backpacks, knowing that she would have wanted Katniss to have them.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_18_33-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_18-33"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>33<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Locating_Peeta">Locating Peeta</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D13&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>By the time of the announcement that two tributes from the same district can win, Katniss's left ear is completely deafened. She cries out for <a href="/wiki/Peeta" class="mw-redirect" title="Peeta">Peeta</a>,<sup id="cite_ref-THG_18_33-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_18-33"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>33<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> but immediately regrets it, realizing the potential danger. She seeks out Peeta and finds him hiding in the mud of a riverbank, totally camouflaged. He's suffering from a severe cut on his leg that <a href="/wiki/Cato" title="Cato">Cato</a> gave him, tracker jacker stings, and multiple burns. She washes him off at the river and treats the burns and stings. Unable to fully treat his leg, she stuffs leaves into it, drawing out infection and pus.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_19_34-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_19-34"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>34<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Katniss does everything she can to care for him, but he eventually gets blood poisoning.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_20_35-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_20-35"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>35<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="The_Feast">The Feast</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D14&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<figure class="thumb mw-halign-left show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 180px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/8/8c/ImagesCAIVZA3A.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20120402153628" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss kisses  in the cave to gain the support of sponsors" src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="180" height="122" class="thumbimage lazyload" data-image-name="ImagesCAIVZA3A.jpg" data-image-key="ImagesCAIVZA3A.jpg" data-relevant="0" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/8/8c/ImagesCAIVZA3A.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20120402153628"></a> 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/8/8c/ImagesCAIVZA3A.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20120402153628" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss kisses  in the cave to gain the support of sponsors" src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/8/8c/ImagesCAIVZA3A.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20120402153628" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="180" height="122" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="ImagesCAIVZA3A.jpg" data-image-key="ImagesCAIVZA3A.jpg" data-relevant="0" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/8/8c/ImagesCAIVZA3A.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20120402153628"></a></noscript> 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:ImagesCAIVZA3A.jpg" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Katniss kisses <a href="/wiki/Peeta_Mellark" title="Peeta Mellark">Peeta</a> in the cave to gain the support of sponsors</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<p>A feast is then announced by the Gamemakers near the Cornucopia, where Katniss will find the item she needs most: the medicine needed to cure Peeta's blood poisoning. Knowing that going to the feast will be dangerous, Katniss insists on going, though Peeta refuses to let her. Immediately afterward, Katniss receives sleeping syrup from their sponsors and tricks Peeta into consuming it by mixing the sleep syrup with berries, telling him it's "sugar berries."<sup id="cite_ref-THG_20_35-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_20-35"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>35<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Precisely after eating the berries, he passes out for 24 hours, giving Katniss enough time to go to the feast and get the medicine for his leg.
</p><p>The feast results in a fight between Katniss and Clove. First, Katniss's forehead gets cut by a knife that <a href="/wiki/Clove" title="Clove">Clove</a> throws at her. Clove pins Katniss to the ground, taunting Katniss about Rue's death and Peeta's predicament. Clove tells her that <a href="/wiki/Cato" title="Cato">Cato</a> let her "have Katniss," but made her promise to give the audience a "good show". Just as she is about to make the first cut at Katniss' lip, Clove is hurled away from her by <a href="/wiki/Thresh" title="Thresh">Thresh</a>, the boy from Rue's district, who slams a large rock into her head after accusing her of "cutting up" <a href="/wiki/Rue" title="Rue">Rue</a>. Katniss is afraid of what Thresh will do to her, but because of her alliance with Rue, he allows her to escape. He does not want to owe Katniss anymore and states that they are now even. She flees the feast with her life and returns to the cave to heal both <a href="/wiki/Peeta" class="mw-redirect" title="Peeta">Peeta</a> and her own wounds.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_21_36-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_21-36"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>36<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_22_10-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_22-10"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>10<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>While they are both recovering, they see that Thresh has been presumably killed at Cato's hands.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_23_37-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_23-37"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>37<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> During one night of rain, Katniss asks Peeta when he started loving her. Peeta tells her it was their first day of school when he heard Katniss sing the Valley Song.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_22_10-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_22-10"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>10<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> After the storm ended, the two go hunting. When Katniss whistles for Peeta and he does not answer, she runs towards him and the two argue until a cannon fires. Peeta had gathered deadly <a href="/wiki/Nightlock_berries" class="mw-redirect" title="Nightlock berries">nightlock berries</a> that he didn't know were poisonous. Katniss expects Peeta to die, but it appears he hasn't eaten any yet. They see <a href="/wiki/Foxface" class="mw-redirect" title="Foxface">Foxface</a>'s body being taken away.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_23_37-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_23-37"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>37<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> A few days later, the river is found empty, as well as the ponds. They figure out that the Gamemakers want them to go to the lake.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_24_38-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_24-38"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>38<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span id="Cato.27s_Death"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Cato's_Death">Cato's Death</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D15&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>When they make it there, they wait a few hours for <a href="/wiki/Cato" title="Cato">Cato</a>, the only other remaining tribute, to arrive. They are about to give up when he runs towards them. When Katniss sees that he is running away from a pack of <a href="/wiki/Wolf_mutt" title="Wolf mutt">wolf mutts</a><sup id="cite_ref-THG_24_38-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_24-38"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>38<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, she and Peeta immediately run with him toward the Cornucopia. They reach the Cornucopia and scale it, though one of the mutts manages to injure Peeta. Once at the top of the horn, Cato finally gets back to his senses and fights Katniss. He overpowers the duo and strangles Peeta to the edge. In a last effort, Peeta draws an X in blood on the back of Cato's hand, telling Katniss to shoot there. Katniss shoots Cato on the back of his hand and he releases Peeta. Peeta then pushes him off so he won't attack them again. Cato is later disarmed by the mutts and gnawed at, being left to suffer. After some time, Katniss decides to kill him out of pity, not vengeance. It is then announced that the rule of two tributes winning if they are from the same district has been revoked and so she and Peeta must fight to the death.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_25_3-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_25-3"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<figure class="thumb  show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 180px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/0/0d/Katniss_peeta_winners.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20120418195715" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Winners Katniss and  watch as a hovercraft descends to pick them up." src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="180" height="198" class="thumbimage lazyload" data-image-name="Katniss peeta winners.jpg" data-image-key="Katniss_peeta_winners.jpg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/0/0d/Katniss_peeta_winners.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20120418195715"></a> 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/0/0d/Katniss_peeta_winners.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20120418195715" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Winners Katniss and  watch as a hovercraft descends to pick them up." src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/0/0d/Katniss_peeta_winners.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20120418195715" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="180" height="198" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="Katniss peeta winners.jpg" data-image-key="Katniss_peeta_winners.jpg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/0/0d/Katniss_peeta_winners.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20120418195715"></a></noscript> 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:Katniss_peeta_winners.jpg" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Winners Katniss and <a href="/wiki/Peeta_Mellark" title="Peeta Mellark">Peeta</a> watch as a hovercraft descends to pick them up.</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Victory">Victory</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D16&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>They pitifully realize that the former rule was just a ruse. Katniss aims her bow at Peeta when she sees he has picked up a weapon, but immediately drops it when he proceeds to throw it into the lake. Feeling guilty, she then takes out some of the nightlock berries and gives a handful to Peeta, and the two of them threaten to commit double suicide so there will be no winner.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_25_3-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_25-3"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<figure class="thumb mw-halign-left show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 250px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/a/a2/Katniss_snow_crown.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20120418193200" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss is crowned victor." src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="250" height="190" class="thumbimage lazyload" data-image-name="Katniss snow crown.jpg" data-image-key="Katniss_snow_crown.jpg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/a/a2/Katniss_snow_crown.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/250?cb=20120418193200"></a> 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/a/a2/Katniss_snow_crown.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20120418193200" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss is crowned victor." src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/a/a2/Katniss_snow_crown.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/250?cb=20120418193200" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="250" height="190" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="Katniss snow crown.jpg" data-image-key="Katniss_snow_crown.jpg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/a/a2/Katniss_snow_crown.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/250?cb=20120418193200"></a></noscript> 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:Katniss_snow_crown.jpg" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Katniss is crowned victor.</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<p>They later discover the Capitol sees this as an act of rebellion<sup id="cite_ref-THG_26_30-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_26-30"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>30<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_2_39-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_2-39"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>39<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. The plan works and both her and Peeta are crowned victors of the <a href="/wiki/74th_Hunger_Games" title="74th Hunger Games">74th Hunger Games</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_25_3-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_25-3"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> After their final interviews and dinner, they board a train that takes them home. Katniss is confused about her feelings for Peeta and becomes even more confused the closer they get to home.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_26_30-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_26-30"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>30<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_27_32-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_27-32"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>32<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="The_Aftermath">The Aftermath</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D17&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<h4><span id="Victors.27_Village"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Victors'_Village">Victors' Village</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D18&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>In <i><a href="/wiki/Catching_Fire" title="Catching Fire">Catching Fire</a></i>, Katniss is back in District 12 living in the <a href="/wiki/Victors%27_Village" title="Victors' Village">Victors' Village</a> with her mother, Prim, and <a href="/wiki/Buttercup" title="Buttercup">Buttercup</a>, and neighbors Peeta and Haymitch. Soon, she and <a href="/wiki/Peeta" class="mw-redirect" title="Peeta">Peeta</a> will have to leave again for the Victory Tour where the winners of the previous Hunger Games travel to each district and make various appearances and public speeches. During much of the day, Katniss is bored with her new life, because now she has everything: money, a good home, and a constant supply of food.
</p>
<figure class="thumb  show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 285px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/c/cb/Katnissandsnow.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20131127062241" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss' conversation with ." src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="285" height="116" class="thumbimage lazyload" data-image-name="Katnissandsnow.jpg" data-image-key="Katnissandsnow.jpg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/c/cb/Katnissandsnow.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/285?cb=20131127062241"></a> 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/c/cb/Katnissandsnow.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20131127062241" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss' conversation with ." src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/c/cb/Katnissandsnow.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/285?cb=20131127062241" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="285" height="116" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="Katnissandsnow.jpg" data-image-key="Katnissandsnow.jpg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/c/cb/Katnissandsnow.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/285?cb=20131127062241"></a></noscript> 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:Katnissandsnow.jpg" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Katniss' conversation with <a href="/wiki/Coriolanus_Snow" title="Coriolanus Snow">President Snow</a>.</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<p>One day after Katniss returns home from hunting game outside of the district, she is visited by <a href="/wiki/Coriolanus_Snow" title="Coriolanus Snow">President Snow</a>, the president of <a href="/wiki/Panem" title="Panem">Panem</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_1_8-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_1-8"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>8<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>  He explains to Katniss that during the last Hunger Games when she tried to commit double suicide with Peeta by eating poisonous nightlock, she ignited a possible uprising in the districts. President Snow and Katniss have a lengthy conversation about the fact that if she doesn't convince the districts that she made the decision out of temporary lovesickness, then he will personally make sure she suffers.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_2_39-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_2-39"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>39<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Katniss, now convinced she has started a fire she cannot control, tries her best to convince everyone that she and Peeta are madly in love<sup id="cite_ref-CF_3_13-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_3-13"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. When she and Peeta finally set off for the <a href="/wiki/Victory_Tour" title="Victory Tour">Victory Tour</a>, everything is going smoothly and Peeta has agreed with Katniss that outside of being star-crossed lovers, they should try to be friends.
</p>
<figure class="thumb mw-halign-left show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 275px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/d/d3/The-hunger-games-catching-fire-gallery.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20131127062733" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss and   giving a speech to ." src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="275" height="115" class="thumbimage lazyload" data-image-name="The-hunger-games-catching-fire-gallery.jpg" data-image-key="The-hunger-games-catching-fire-gallery.jpg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/d/d3/The-hunger-games-catching-fire-gallery.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/275?cb=20131127062733"></a> 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/d/d3/The-hunger-games-catching-fire-gallery.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20131127062733" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss and   giving a speech to ." src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/d/d3/The-hunger-games-catching-fire-gallery.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/275?cb=20131127062733" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="275" height="115" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="The-hunger-games-catching-fire-gallery.jpg" data-image-key="The-hunger-games-catching-fire-gallery.jpg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/d/d3/The-hunger-games-catching-fire-gallery.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/275?cb=20131127062733"></a></noscript> 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:The-hunger-games-catching-fire-gallery.jpg" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Katniss and <a href="/wiki/Peeta_Mellark" title="Peeta Mellark">Peeta</a>  giving a speech to <a href="/wiki/District_11" title="District 11">District 11</a>.</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<p>When the tour arrives at their first destination, District 11<sup id="cite_ref-CF_4_40-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_4-40"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>40<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, Katniss tells everyone the story of how Thresh spared her life<sup id="cite_ref-THG_21_36-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_21-36"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>36<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, and how Rue became a valued ally during the Games<sup id="cite_ref-CF_41-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF-41"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>41<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. After her speech, an old man whistles her and Rue's four-note tune that meant they were safe, and that signals the end of the workday in <a href="/wiki/District_11" title="District 11">District 11</a>, the crowd simultaneously does a traditional <a href="/wiki/District_12" title="District 12">District 12</a> gesture to show their thanks to Katniss and as a small act of rebellion. Soon after Katniss finishes her speech, a Peacekeeper shoots the man who whistled in the head, along with two others.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_4_40-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_4-40"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>40<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Near the end of the Tour, they visit the Capitol and when she and Peeta are being interviewed by Caesar, Peeta performs a fake proposal to Katniss, which she accepts. President Snow makes a surprise visit to congratulate Katniss and <a href="/wiki/Peeta" class="mw-redirect" title="Peeta">Peeta</a>. He motions to Katniss that she was unsuccessful in convincing their love.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_5_31-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_5-31"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>31<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> President Snow decides to hold their wedding in the Capitol. There is a party held in the banquet room of his mansion. Katniss there learns that in order to taste each and every dish the people of Capitol drink a clear liquid which causes them to vomit and empty their stomachs in order to continue eating and do this again and again. This disturbs her since she had seen her mother treating emaciated children whose parents didn't have anything to afford for them, not even their medicine, and she was also poor before winning the games and had almost starved to death once. She is introduced to <a href="/wiki/Plutarch_Heavensbee" title="Plutarch Heavensbee">Plutarch Heavensbee</a>, the new Head Gamemaker by <a href="/wiki/Portia" title="Portia">Portia</a>. They then travel to <a href="/wiki/District_12" title="District 12">District 12</a>. Katniss suffers from nightmares throughout the night on the train, so Peeta sleeps in her bed to calm her and keeps her company. Finally, they get back to <a href="/wiki/District_12" title="District 12">District 12</a> and a huge party is thrown for them at Mayor Undersee's house. While in the mayor's home, Katniss sees on his television that there is an uprising going on in <a href="/wiki/District_8" title="District 8">District 8</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_6_42-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_6-42"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>42<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Katniss arranges a secret meeting with Gale, during which he confesses his love for her. <a href="/wiki/Gale" class="mw-redirect" title="Gale">Gale</a> gets angry and disappointed but before he leaves, Katniss accidentally tells him there was a rebellion in <a href="/wiki/District_8" title="District 8">District 8</a>. Gale then says that they could have a rebellion in District 12 as well, and he runs off to spread the news.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_7_43-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_7-43"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>43<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span id="Gale.27s_Whipping"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Gale's_Whipping">Gale's Whipping</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D19&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>Katniss then goes to <a href="/wiki/Peeta" class="mw-redirect" title="Peeta">Peeta</a> to ask him if he would run away with her and he agrees to go with her, but while they are discussing the plans, they see that Gale is being violently whipped in the square by an unfamiliar man<sup id="cite_ref-CF_7_43-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_7-43"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>43<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>.
</p><p>Katniss steps in to stop the man, whose name is <a href="/wiki/Romulus_Thread" title="Romulus Thread">Romulus Thread</a>, the Head Peacekeeper but he gives her a lash across the face. Katniss gets severely hurt.
