Eventually, the people of District 12, including many who frequent the Hob, convince Thread to stop whipping <a href="/wiki/Gale" class="mw-redirect" title="Gale">Gale</a>. Haymitch steps in and holds her up and shouts at Romulus since she has a photo-shoot next week.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_8_44-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_8-44"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>44<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> In the movie, <a href="/wiki/Haymitch" class="mw-redirect" title="Haymitch">Haymitch</a> and <a href="/wiki/Peeta" class="mw-redirect" title="Peeta">Peeta</a> step in to save her.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_41-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF-41"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>41<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Purnia, a female commoner, steps in, saying the proper amount of lashes has been given. Romulus warns all of them and leaves with the Peacekeepers including <a href="/wiki/Darius" title="Darius">Darius</a>, a male Peacekeeper who had been hit on the head by Thread and was lying unconscious. They manage to pick up Gale on a countertop provided by an old woman in her clothing stall. Katniss tells Leevy, a former neighbor, to call Hazelle, Gale's mother. <a href="/wiki/Bristel" title="Bristel">Bristel</a> and <a href="/wiki/Thom" title="Thom">Thom</a>, Gale's crewmembers from the mine told everyone the situation. Gale had killed a wild turkey and went to <a href="/wiki/Cray" title="Cray">Cray</a>, the previous Head Peacekeeper to bid for it. Cray was lenient, alcoholic, and overlooked such crimes. But he was replaced by Romulus Thread and Gale didn't have any idea about it. Expecting Cray to pay him, he went to his house and found Thread there. Gale could not say much in his defense and was punished with whiplashes.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_8_44-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_8-44"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>44<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Darius, a Peacekeeper who was unconscious had stepped in first after 20 lashes saying that it was enough but didn't do it as smartly and officially as <a href="/wiki/Purnia" title="Purnia">Purnia</a> did and grabbed Thread's arm. In return, Thread hit him on his head with the butt of the whip forming a large purple lump on his head, making him unconscious.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_8_44-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_8-44"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>44<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Gale received forty lashes until Katniss showed up and he passed out at around thirty. Katniss and the others take Gale to her mother. Katniss's mother tries to soothe the pain by giving him medicine. Hazelle also comes there and sits beside her son. Madge brings her mother's box of vials containing <a href="/wiki/Morphling_(drug)" title="Morphling (drug)">morphling</a>, a kind of medicine. Mrs. Everdeen injects it into Gale. Later on that night, Katniss says she picks Gale but is still torn between him and Peeta.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_8_44-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_8-44"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>44<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Katniss has a nightmare in which Clove turns into a mutt. She wakes up with a start, shivering. The conditions in <a href="/wiki/District_12" title="District 12">District 12</a> worsen. People are punished for their crimes, mines are shut down for two weeks, etc.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_9_45-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_9-45"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>45<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Bonnie_and_Twill">Bonnie and Twill</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D20&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>Katniss realizes that she cannot stand to be at home, so she goes into the woods and heads to the lake<sup id="cite_ref-CF_9_45-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_9-45"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>45<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. There, she meets two women named <a href="/wiki/Bonnie" title="Bonnie">Bonnie</a> and <a href="/wiki/Twill" title="Twill">Twill</a> who have run away from District 8. They say that they are headed to <a href="/wiki/District_13" title="District 13">District 13</a> since they believe that the district was not actually destroyed and there are people living there underground, and that the uprising in District 8 was a planned one<sup id="cite_ref-CF_10_46-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_10-46"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>46<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. Their evidence was the fact that the Capitol always shows old footage of a destroyed <a href="/wiki/District_13" title="District 13">District 13</a> from 75 years ago; every time it airs, the same Mockingjay's wing can be seen for a brief second in the corner.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_11_47-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_11-47"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>47<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>.
</p><p>Katniss teaches them the essentials of surviving in a forest and gives them food to eat. When the evening comes, they leave, and Katniss returns to <a href="/wiki/District_12" title="District 12">District 12</a><sup id="cite_ref-CF_10_46-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_10-46"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>46<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. However, she realizes that the fence to the district is now electrified and so she must climb a tree and jump from 25 feet in the air. She lands and hurts her tailbone and heel. She returns home to tell her mother how she fell after slipping on a patch of ice, but there are two Peacekeepers there waiting for her. They are surprised to see her because they knew she was in the forest and believed she would be trapped on the other side of the fence. They were waiting for her not to show up so they can bring her family in for questioning. Luckily, Haymitch, Peeta, and Prim were able to go along with a story to cover for her.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_11_47-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_11-47"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>47<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="The_Third_Quarter_Quell">The Third Quarter Quell</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D21&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Preparation">Preparation</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D22&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>Once she is healed, her prep team comes to help with her wedding fashion shoot, along with Cinna. She listens to them speak of how they have not had shrimp in the Capitol for weeks and realizes that other districts are rebelling. The next night, there is a view of the photo-shoot and a voting on which was the best dress and later there is an important announcement made by President Snow: in honor of the third <a href="/wiki/Quarter_Quell" title="Quarter Quell">Quarter Quell</a>, for the <a href="/wiki/75th_Hunger_Games" title="75th Hunger Games">75th Hunger Games</a> the tributes will be reaped from the existing pool of victors. Since <a href="/wiki/District_12" title="District 12">District 12</a> has only three living winners, <a href="/wiki/Peeta" class="mw-redirect" title="Peeta">Peeta</a>, <a href="/wiki/Haymitch" class="mw-redirect" title="Haymitch">Haymitch</a>, and Katniss, and being the only female victor, she knows that she is now going to be forced to go back into the arena.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_12_4-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_12-4"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>She runs out of the house and into a basement of an empty one to scream, cry, and proceeds to shatter a window of the kitchen. This makes her hand bleed. She makes her way to <a href="/wiki/Haymitch" class="mw-redirect" title="Haymitch">Haymitch</a>, where he already tells her Peeta has come to visit him and told him that Peeta wants to go back into the arena to protect Katniss. She tells Haymitch that whatever they do, she wants Haymitch to make sure Peeta comes back alive. She and <a href="/wiki/Haymitch" class="mw-redirect" title="Haymitch">Haymitch</a> get drunk. She returns in an inebriated state to her house and sees Gale at the doorway and Katniss passes out in his arms. She has a horrible hangover the next day and, in the afternoon, she goes back to Haymitch's house, where Peeta has emptied all of his liquor and has threatened to turn them or anyone who gives them alcohol in. He tells them that they will be training for the Quarter Quell because two people will come back alive, and they begin to work out and study footage of past winners to see their competition.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_13_48-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_13-48"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>48<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="The_Reaping_2">The Reaping</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D23&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<figure class="thumb mw-halign-right show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 222px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/2/22/Catching-fire-effie-katniss-district-11-1000-621x350.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20131127062926" class="mw-file-description image"><img 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/2/22/Catching-fire-effie-katniss-district-11-1000-621x350.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20131127062926" class="mw-file-description
