	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:Catching-fire-effie-katniss-district-11-1000-621x350.jpg" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption"><a href="/wiki/Peeta_Mellark" title="Peeta Mellark">Peeta</a> and Katniss at <a href="/wiki/District_12" title="District 12">District 12</a> before heading to the train.</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<p>On the day of the reaping, Katniss is the only choice for the female competitor, and the chosen male competitor is Haymitch. Peeta, however, volunteers to take his place. Instead of being able to tell their families goodbye, they are brought directly to the train and leave for the Capitol.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_13_48-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_13-48"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>48<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Tribute_Parade">Tribute Parade</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D24&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>Katniss's prep team prepares her for the Tribute Parade, but they all are very tearful and upset. When Cinna comes in with her outfit, she tells him that if he cries, she will kill him right there. She then sees her outfit, which looks like coal burning in a fireplace. Cinna tells her to look above it all and not wave or smile. When she is next to her chariot, <a href="/wiki/Finnick_Odair" title="Finnick Odair">Finnick Odair</a> walks up to her and flirtatiously offers her a sugar cube, which she turns down. They have a short, uncomfortable conversation. During the parade, Katniss sees that other districts have copied Cinna's idea of light in the outfits, although not as well as Cinna did.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_15_49-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_15-49"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>49<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>After the parade, she gets into the elevator with other tributes. <a href="/wiki/Johanna_Mason" title="Johanna Mason">Johanna Mason</a> is in the elevator with them and she strips, expressing disgust for her own costume while initiating casual conversation with Peeta. Later, Peeta tells her Finnick and Johanna played with Katniss because she seemed so "pure."<sup id="cite_ref-CF_15_49-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_15-49"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>49<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Training_Sessions_2">Training Sessions</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D25&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>Once at the Capitol, they eat and meet their competitors.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_15_49-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_15-49"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>49<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> During training, Katniss befriends <a href="/wiki/Wiress" title="Wiress">Wiress</a> and <a href="/wiki/Beetee_Latier" title="Beetee Latier">Beetee Latier</a> from <a href="/wiki/District_3" title="District 3">District 3</a>, who point out the force field used to separate the Gamemakers from the tributes. In her solo performance for the Gamemakers, Katniss sees <a href="/wiki/Plutarch_Heavensbee" title="Plutarch Heavensbee">Plutarch Heavensbee</a>, the new Head Gamemaker, and decides to tie a noose around a dummy's neck. With berry juice, she writes the name "<a href="/wiki/Seneca_Crane" title="Seneca Crane">Seneca Crane</a>," the former Head Gamemaker who was killed by orders of President Snow.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_16_50-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_16-50"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>50<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> She and Peeta both receive perfect 12s, which Haymitch explains was done to make sure the other tributes are gunning for both of them<sup id="cite_ref-CF_17_51-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_17-51"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>51<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>.
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Interviews">Interviews</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D26&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<figure class="thumb mw-halign-left show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 222px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/f/f3/Katnissmockingjay.png/revision/latest?cb=20130720220506" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss in her mockingjay outfit." src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="222" height="141" class="thumbimage lazyload" data-image-name="Katnissmockingjay.png" data-image-key="Katnissmockingjay.png" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/f/f3/Katnissmockingjay.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/222?cb=20130720220506"></a> 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/f/f3/Katnissmockingjay.png/revision/latest?cb=20130720220506" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss in her mockingjay outfit." src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/f/f3/Katnissmockingjay.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/222?cb=20130720220506" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="222" height="141" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="Katnissmockingjay.png" data-image-key="Katnissmockingjay.png" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/f/f3/Katnissmockingjay.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/222?cb=20130720220506"></a></noscript> 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:Katnissmockingjay.png" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Katniss in her mockingjay outfit.</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<p>On the day of the interview, the prep team comes into Katniss' room, and they start to cry; they leave one by one. Then, only <a href="/wiki/Venia" title="Venia">Venia</a> is left. She does the job of the whole team. At last, <a href="/wiki/Cinna" title="Cinna">Cinna</a> comes in with her wedding gown from the photoshoot, specifically chosen by <a href="/wiki/Coriolanus_Snow" title="Coriolanus Snow">Coriolanus Snow</a>. She notes to <a href="/wiki/Cinna" title="Cinna">Cinna</a> that it's heavier than before, but he explains that he had to do some alterations and tells her not to lift her arms until she twirls on television. The interview atmosphere is different from the previous year. The tributes are speaking out against the Capitol, asking why President Snow is not stopping this if he is great and powerful. When Katniss comes on stage with the dress on, apologizing to the crowd for not letting them see her get married. She twirls and raises her arms and the wedding dress burns away to reveal a different dress underneath. When she looks towards a television, she realizes that Cinna has turned her into a mockingjay. <sup id="cite_ref-CF_17_51-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_17-51"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>51<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Although Caesar knows what the dress represents, they play it off as the bird on Katniss' token. When it comes to Peeta's turn to speak, he lies and tells the audience that he and Katniss are already married, and that Katniss is pregnant. This drives the audience to madness and hysteria.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_18_52-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_18-52"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>52<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Cornucopia_bloodbath_2">Cornucopia bloodbath</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D27&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<figure class="thumb  show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 222px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/7/70/The_hunger_games_catching_fire-still_2.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20131127063323" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss in the arena." src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="222" height="120" class="thumbimage lazyload" data-image-name="The hunger games catching fire-still 2.jpg" data-image-key="The_hunger_games_catching_fire-still_2.jpg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/7/70/The_hunger_games_catching_fire-still_2.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/222?cb=20131127063323"></a> 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/7/70/The_hunger_games_catching_fire-still_2.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20131127063323" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss in the arena." src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/7/70/The_hunger_games_catching_fire-still_2.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/222?cb=20131127063323" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="222" height="120" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="The hunger games catching fire-still 2.jpg" data-image-key="The_hunger_games_catching_fire-still_2.jpg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/7/70/The_hunger_games_catching_fire-still_2.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/222?cb=20131127063323"></a></noscript> 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:The_hunger_games_catching_fire-still_2.jpg" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Katniss in the arena.</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<p>Right before the Games are to begin, Katniss is with <a href="/wiki/Cinna" title="Cinna">Cinna</a> and steps on the plate to be taken to the arena, where she is frozen in place. Suddenly three Peacekeepers enter the room, beat Cinna, and drag him out of the room. A frozen Katniss watches in horror but is then transported into the arena.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_18_52-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_18-52"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>52<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> In <i>Mockingjay</i>, it is revealed that Cinna was actually killed as a result of his injuries. The plate leaves her in the middle of blue water, which she realizes "is no place for a girl on fire".<sup id="cite_ref-CF_18_52-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_18-52"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>52<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> While retrieving a bow and arrows, she sees that <a href="/wiki/Finnick_Odair" title="Finnick Odair">Finnick</a> is wearing a gold bracelet<sup id="cite_ref-CF_19_53-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_19-53"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>53<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> that once belonged to Haymitch<sup id="cite_ref-CF_16_50-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_16-50"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>50<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> and realizes that this was his way of telling her to form an alliance with Finnick. She and <a href="/wiki/Peeta_Mellark" title="Peeta Mellark">Peeta</a> run into the jungle with Finnick, who carries his 80-year-old district partner, <a href="/wiki/Mags" class="mw-redirect" title="Mags">Mags</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_19_53-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_19-53"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>53<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Saving_Peeta">Saving Peeta</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D28&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>As they are walking, searching for drinkable water, Katniss notices a shimmering square and realizes that it is a <a href="/wiki/Force_field" title="Force field">force field</a> surrounding the arena. Before she can stop him, <a href="/wiki/Peeta_Mellark" title="Peeta Mellark">Peeta</a> walks into it and is repelled back, knocking Mags and <a href="/wiki/Finnick_Odair" title="Finnick Odair">Finnick</a> to the ground. Peeta is not breathing.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_19_53-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_19-53"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>53<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Panicked, Katniss begins to slap him, but his heart has stopped. Finnick helps by performing CPR and Peeta is eventually resuscitated. Finnick asks Katniss how she recognized the force field. She does not want to let them know she can spot them visually, worried that the Gamemakers might do something to retaliate, so she tells them she heard it with her repaired ear.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_20_54-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_20-54"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>54<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Escaping_the_fog">Escaping the fog</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D29&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>Later that day she notices an unnatural, and poisonous <a href="/wiki/Fog" title="Fog">fog</a> in the distance. The mist from the fog causes blisters where it touches.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_20_54-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_20-54"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>54<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> When Peeta stumbles, she notices it also attacks the nerves, as his face droops and his legs stop working. Finnick agrees to carry Peeta if Katniss can carry Mags, which she agrees to. But after a while, Katniss cannot carry Mags anymore as her legs become paralyzed. Finnick comes back and tells Katniss he cannot carry them both. Mags kisses Finnick and runs into the fog, convulsing and dying.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_21_55-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_21-55"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>55<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Throughout the early morning, Katniss, Peeta, and Finnick help each other as they meet different challenges in the arena.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_22_56-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_22-56"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>56<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_21_55-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_21-55"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>55<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_23_57-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_23-57"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>57<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_24_58-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_24-58"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>58<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>.
</p>
<h4><span id="Alliance_with_Johanna.2C_Wiress.2C_and_Beetee"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Alliance_with_Johanna,_Wiress,_and_Beetee">Alliance with Johanna, Wiress, and Beetee</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D30&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>Later, they meet <a href="/wiki/Johanna_Mason" title="Johanna Mason">Johanna Mason</a>, whom <a href="/wiki/Finnick" class="mw-redirect" title="Finnick">Finnick</a> runs to, and they hug. As Katniss and <a href="/wiki/Peeta" class="mw-redirect" title="Peeta">Peeta</a> figure out what to do, they realize they cannot leave Finnick after all he has done and go to <a href="/wiki/Johanna" class="mw-redirect" title="Johanna">Johanna</a> instead. She has <a href="/wiki/Wiress" title="Wiress">Wiress</a> and <a href="/wiki/Beetee" class="mw-redirect" title="Beetee">Beetee</a> with her. Beetee has received a knife in the back, while Wiress is spinning in a circle saying, "Tick tock, tick tock," repeatedly. Johanna explains that Haymitch told her to bring them to Katniss for an alliance. Later that night, when Wiress restates "tick tock," Katniss begins to realize the arena is in the shape of a clock.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_22_56-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_22-56"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>56<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> She also remembers <a href="/wiki/Plutarch_Heavensbee" title="Plutarch Heavensbee">Plutarch Heavensbee</a> showing her his clock watch and saying, "It begins at midnight," as an image of a mockingjay flashed on the watch face.
</p><p>They go to the Cornucopia to see if they are correct and are proven to be right. While drawing up the map of the clock, Katniss realizes that <a href="/wiki/Wiress" title="Wiress">Wiress</a> has stopped singing because <a href="/wiki/Gloss" title="Gloss">Gloss</a> slit her throat and turns with an arrow ready and hits Gloss in the temple, while <a href="/wiki/Johanna" class="mw-redirect" title="Johanna">Johanna</a> buries an axe in <a href="/wiki/Cashmere" title="Cashmere">Cashmere</a>'s chest. Katniss starts to realize that everyone is protecting <a href="/wiki/Peeta" class="mw-redirect" title="Peeta">Peeta</a> and wonders if they believe that he can be the leader of the rebellion.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_23_57-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_23-57"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>57<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Later, Peeta tries to give Katniss a gold locket and tells her he wants her to win, because if she dies, he has nothing to live for. They kiss, and she realizes that she loves Peeta<sup id="cite_ref-CF_24_58-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_24-58"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>58<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> and wants to fulfill her plan of keeping him alive<sup id="cite_ref-CF_25_59-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_25-59"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>59<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>.
</p>
<h4><span id="Beetee.27s_Plan"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Beetee's_Plan">Beetee's Plan</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D31&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>The next day, after telling <a href="/wiki/Peeta" class="mw-redirect" title="Peeta">Peeta</a> that they need to leave the alliance, he tells her to wait because he believes Beetee has a plan. The plan is to tie Beetee's wire around a tree that is struck by lightning and run the wire into the ocean, making the beach a conductor to electrocute everything in its path.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_25_59-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_25-59"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>59<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Katniss and Johanna are in charge of running the wire down to the beach because they are the fastest, however, they are attacked by <a href="/wiki/Brutus" title="Brutus">Brutus</a> and <a href="/wiki/Enobaria" title="Enobaria">Enobaria</a> of District 2. Katniss is knocked in the head and through her blurred vision sees <a href="/wiki/Johanna" class="mw-redirect" title="Johanna">Johanna</a> on top of her stabbing her arm and telling her to stay down.
</p><p>Katniss hears Finnick running but stays quiet so as not to alert him to her whereabouts, since she believes that he and Johanna have betrayed them. Realizing that Beetee was trying to blow up the forcefield, she wraps his wire around her bow and shoots it straight into the flaw in the forcefield at the exact moment when the lightning strikes the tree, blowing up the arena.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_26_60-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_26-60"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>60<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> After she is carried out of the arena and transported into a hovercraft, she lays there realizing that the Capitol will not let anyone live after this and sees Plutarch Heavensbee coming over to her as she passes out<sup id="cite_ref-CF_27_7-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_27-7"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>.
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="After_the_Quarter_Quell">After the Quarter Quell</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D32&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="District_13">District 13</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D33&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>She awakes in a hospital bed next to Beetee. She searches for Peeta, believing that the Capitol has captured them, and planning to kill Peeta to save him from being tortured. She blames Haymitch because he promised that he would save Peeta instead of her.
</p><p>She passes a room and hears Plutarch talking to Finnick and Haymitch about getting someone out of District 4. She enters the room and Haymitch tells her that there was a plan to break them out the minute the Quell was announced. The victors from districts 3, 4, 6, 7, 8, and 11 had varying degrees of knowledge about it. Plutarch had been for several years part of a group planning to overthrow the Capitol. The hovercraft they are in is from District 13, which is where they are headed. She tells them that Johanna tried to kill her, but Finnick explains that she was removing Katniss's <a href="/wiki/Tracker" title="Tracker">tracker</a>, and that all of the victor tributes in those districts have pledged their lives to her and the rebellion; that she is the Mockingjay, the symbol of the rebellion.
</p><p>She realizes that Haymitch had lied to both her and Peeta. He tells her that everyone kept Peeta alive because they knew that if he died, she would never keep an alliance with the others. She asks where Peeta is, and Haymitch tells her he was picked up, along with Johanna and Enobaria, by the Capitol. In horror and anger, she attacks Haymitch, scratching his face. Finnick and others strap her down and drug her to keep her calm. Finnick tells her that the Capitol will at least keep Peeta alive to use as bait against Katniss.
</p><p>Later, Gale visits her. He tells her that after the Games, the Capitol sent bombers to District 12. He explains that he was able to get her family out in time, but District 12 has been destroyed.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_27_7-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_27-7"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="District_13_Rebellion">District 13 Rebellion</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D34&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<div class="quote">
<dl><dd><span title="Source: The Hunger Games: Mockingjay - Part 2">"<i>Tonight, turn your weapons to <a href="/wiki/The_Capitol" title="The Capitol">the Capitol</a>! Turn your weapons to <a href="/wiki/Coriolanus_Snow" title="Coriolanus Snow">Snow</a>!</i>"</span></dd>
<dd>—Katniss Everdeen to the citizens of <a href="/wiki/Panem" title="Panem">Panem</a>.<sup class="noprint"><a href="/wiki/The_Hunger_Games:_Mockingjay_-_Part_2" title="The Hunger Games: Mockingjay - Part 2">[src]</a></sup></dd></dl></div>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Visiting_District_12">Visiting District 12</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D35&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<figure class="thumb mw-halign-left show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 250px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/0/0d/The_hunger_games_mockingjay_part_1_katniss.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20150109221047" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss in her home, ." src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="250" height="150" class="thumbimage lazyload" data-image-name="The hunger games mockingjay part 1 katniss.jpg" data-image-key="The_hunger_games_mockingjay_part_1_katniss.jpg" data-relevant="0" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/0/0d/The_hunger_games_mockingjay_part_1_katniss.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/250?cb=20150109221047"></a> 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/0/0d/The_hunger_games_mockingjay_part_1_katniss.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20150109221047" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss in her home, ." src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/0/0d/The_hunger_games_mockingjay_part_1_katniss.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/250?cb=20150109221047" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="250" height="150" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="The hunger games mockingjay part 1 katniss.jpg" data-image-key="The_hunger_games_mockingjay_part_1_katniss.jpg" data-relevant="0" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/0/0d/The_hunger_games_mockingjay_part_1_katniss.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/250?cb=20150109221047"></a></noscript> 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:The_hunger_games_mockingjay_part_1_katniss.jpg" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Katniss in her home, <a href="/wiki/District_12" title="District 12">District 12</a>.</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<p>In <i><a href="/wiki/Mockingjay" title="Mockingjay">Mockingjay</a></i>, Katniss is in what's left of District 13 Underground. She goes to visit the ruins of District 12 after the firebombing from the Capitol. She finds that Buttercup (Prim's cat) somehow survived the destruction of District 12. She takes Buttercup and hides him in her hunting bag as a comforting present to Prim who is in District 13 with her mother and the other residents of District 12 that survived the bombing. While examining her old room, she finds a white rose that was left by President Snow as a sign meaning that he was watching her, possibly right then.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_1_5-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_1-5"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Becoming_the_Mockingjay">Becoming the Mockingjay</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D36&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>Katniss is stressed and overwhelmed by everything that is happening - District 13, wondering if Peeta is still alive, etc.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_1_5-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_1-5"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> -- but is glad she now has her old relationship with Gale. She gets used to life in District 13 and starts to like it -- but she sees District 13 as an underground prison with nowhere to go. Katniss watches Peeta's interview with Caesar Flickerman and is filled with joy that Peeta is still alive and healthy. However, Peeta shockingly declares a cease-fire, claiming that it is the best course of action for the Rebels and the Capitol. The citizens of District 13 watching begin to call Peeta a "traitor". Katniss runs off to a supply closet and hides there alone until Gale enters. The two talk about the situation, and that is when she decides that she will be "the mockingjay," a symbol of the rebellion against the Capitol.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_2_6-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_2-6"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> As part of the deal, she demands that the leader of District 13, <a href="/wiki/Alma_Coin" title="Alma Coin">Alma Coin</a>, grant immunity to all of the victors of the Hunger Games. She demands the right to kill President Snow herself and asks for her family to keep their cat, Buttercup, and to hunt with Gale. After the meeting, Plutarch takes her and Gale downstairs where they are stopped by a guard due to a secret room. Gale makes a distraction, and Katniss rushes in and sees her prep team being held prisoner.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_3_1-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_3-1"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Katniss helps them recover by taking them to her mother for treatment. During the meeting held by President Coin for the announcement of her role and conditions, Katniss sees <a href="/wiki/Finnick" class="mw-redirect" title="Finnick">Finnick</a> distracted and in grief. She calls for him, but he doesn't answer. She shakes him to get his attention, and the two embrace each other. When Katniss is reminded of <a href="/wiki/Annie_Cresta" title="Annie Cresta">Annie</a>, she tells Coin to pencil in Annie as one of the victors to receive immunity. Katniss is surprised by Coin's message during the meeting that, if she doesn't meet all of her obligations as the mockingjay, she and her friends and family are dead.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_4_61-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_4-61"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>61<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Skirmish_in_District_8">Skirmish in District 8</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D37&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>Katniss and Gale go hunting together like the old times, and they both enjoy it until they get in an argument over the fact that she cares for her prep team. Katniss leaves and is angry with Gale<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_4_61-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_4-61"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>61<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. She is being fixed by her prep team to make her look beautiful for the propo she will film as the mockingjay. When they finish, she and Gale go downstairs to the weapons room where Beetee is making weapons for the rebels. After she receives her weapons, she goes to the room where she will shoot her first propaganda, or <a href="/wiki/Propos" class="mw-redirect" title="Propos">propo</a>. After she finishes, Haymitch goes on the speaker and tells them, "That is how you end a revolution."<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_5_62-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_5-62"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>62<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> After a brief meeting, they decide to let Katniss speak for herself and to do it in action. Katniss suits up in her <a href="/wiki/Cinna#Designs" title="Cinna">mockingjay uniform</a> and heads to District 8 to shoot her first propaganda.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_6_63-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_6-63"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>63<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<figure class="thumb  show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 250px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/8/8f/Katniss23.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20150219055946" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss in the  hospital." src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="250" height="150" class="thumbimage lazyload" data-image-name="Katniss23.jpg" data-image-key="Katniss23.jpg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/8/8f/Katniss23.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/250?cb=20150219055946"></a> 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/8/8f/Katniss23.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20150219055946" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss in the  hospital." src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/8/8f/Katniss23.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/250?cb=20150219055946" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="250" height="150" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="Katniss23.jpg" data-image-key="Katniss23.jpg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/8/8f/Katniss23.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/250?cb=20150219055946"></a></noscript> 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:Katniss23.jpg" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Katniss in the <a href="/wiki/District_8" title="District 8">District 8</a> hospital.</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<p>During her visit, the hospital is attacked by the Capitol. The rebel forces fight back and are able to defeat them, but the hospital is destroyed. Katniss speaks her own words after the attack.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_7_64-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_7-64"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>64<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> After viewing the finished propaganda, she sees that it is strong. The group decides to shoot more and to send her back into combat as soon she feels up to it.
</p><p>Katniss has dinner with Finnick in the hospital. Finnick comforts her, and they watch the propaganda again. Katniss tells him to shut the television off but stops him because Caesar is going to interview Peeta. She notes Peeta lost fifteen pounds and watches the interview. In the interview, Peeta asks Katniss why she joined the rebels, and if she didn't know, she should find out.
</p><p>After watching it, she pretends to have not seen it because Plutarch walks in and makes sure she is well.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_8_65-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_8-65"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>65<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> She is overwhelmed by Peeta's interview. Katniss and Finnick go to the woods to hunt in District 13. Katniss talks about Peeta's broadcast from last night and questions why no one is talking about it. Katniss is thankful that Finnick is here to listen to her.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_9_15-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_9-15"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Katniss_and_Gale">Katniss and Gale</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D38&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>The next day, Katniss and Gale have tension between them. Katniss is furious that Gale did not talk or say anything about Peeta's interview. Gale tells her it was to protect her and that he had to lie. Katniss, enraged by this, tells him to leave her alone and to stop lying to her. The next day, Katniss is informed she must go back to District 12 with Gale and the others to shoot more shots for the propaganda. Katniss goes to the remains of her home and feels pain inside of her. Katniss feels more pain when Gale asks to go to his house, but <a href="/wiki/Cressida" title="Cressida">Cressida</a> tells him to react to the night of the bombing.
</p><p>Katniss goes and communicates with an Avox that escaped the Capitol, <a href="/wiki/Pollux" title="Pollux">Pollux</a>. She sings "The Hanging Tree" to him but is cut off by <a href="/wiki/Cressida" title="Cressida">Cressida</a>. Katniss begins to forgive Gale because the two must stay together. Katniss goes back to the Victor village and searches the area. Katniss and Gale kiss because they are both in pain, and the only way to escape it is by doing this.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_9_15-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_9-15"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span id="Peeta.27s_Warning"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Peeta's_Warning">Peeta's Warning</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D39&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>A few days later, Katniss watches another interview with Peeta and becomes overwhelmed. Peeta again calls for a cease-fire, pointing out how much damage has been done to the districs' infrastructures and blaming the rebels for all of it. Suddenly, Beetee hijacks the broadcast and begins playing the propo they filmed in District 12. The propo then suddenly cuts back to Peeta, who tries to continue his speech, but fails. Beetee and the Capitol fight for control of the broadcast as it switches back and forth between Peeta and the propos. Suddenly, the Capitol seal flashes on the screen, accompanied by a "flat audio tone." After about twenty seconds, Peeta and President Snow appear on screen again, this time surrounded by turmoil. Snow tells the camera that the rebels are obviously trying to "disrupt the dissemination of information they find incriminating, but both truth and justice will reign." He then asks Peeta if he has any "parting thoughts for Katniss Everdeen." Peeta then speaks directly to Katniss, saying that nobody is safe in Panem and that she will be dead by morning. Katniss and the others realize that Peeta was warning the rebels that the Capitol was about to attack District 13. Beetee hijacks the broadcast again, flashing a short clip of Katniss standing in front of the destroyed hospital. When Peeta returns to the screen, he tries to continue talking, but the camera is knocked to the floor. Peeta is beaten, and his blood splatters across the screen.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_9_15-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_9-15"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Katniss sees this and goes into shock. She backs up Haymitch's theory that it is a warning of a bombing, and the inhabitants of District 13 retreat to a safe bunker. When Mrs. Everdeen arrives and is not with Prim, Katniss deduces that she went back for Buttercup. She stops the doors from being closed and locked in time for Prim and Gale to enter. Katniss stays with her family in one area, and after the first bombing, Mrs. Everdeen leaves to the first aid station to help heal the wounded. Katniss is worried about Peeta, but Prim comforts her and tells her President Snow wouldn't kill him because he needs him to get to her. Katniss asks what she thinks will happen to Peeta, to which Prim replies, whatever it takes to break her.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_10_66-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_10-66"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>66<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Katniss plays with Buttercup during the night with a flashlight and imagines how she can get Peeta back. She goes to Finnick and shares her ideas on Snow's plan. Finnick tells Katniss he is doing the same but with Annie. The two continue to talk about President Snow's plan.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_11_67-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_11-67"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>67<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Rescue_of_Peeta">Rescue of Peeta</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D40&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<figure class="thumb mw-halign-left show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 220px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/e/e1/Bf5cbb02-653a-11e4-9d3c-bc764e11a0bc.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20141219074249" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss in the remains of ." src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="220" height="147" class="thumbimage lazyload" data-image-name="Bf5cbb02-653a-11e4-9d3c-bc764e11a0bc.jpg" data-image-key="Bf5cbb02-653a-11e4-9d3c-bc764e11a0bc.jpg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/e/e1/Bf5cbb02-653a-11e4-9d3c-bc764e11a0bc.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/220?cb=20141219074249"></a> 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/e/e1/Bf5cbb02-653a-11e4-9d3c-bc764e11a0bc.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20141219074249" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss in the remains of ." src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/e/e1/Bf5cbb02-653a-11e4-9d3c-bc764e11a0bc.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/220?cb=20141219074249" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="220" height="147" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="Bf5cbb02-653a-11e4-9d3c-bc764e11a0bc.jpg" data-image-key="Bf5cbb02-653a-11e4-9d3c-bc764e11a0bc.jpg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/e/e1/Bf5cbb02-653a-11e4-9d3c-bc764e11a0bc.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/220?cb=20141219074249"></a></noscript> 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:Bf5cbb02-653a-11e4-9d3c-bc764e11a0bc.jpg" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Katniss in the remains of <a href="/wiki/District_12" title="District 12">District 12</a>.</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<p>Katniss, while shooting a propaganda, announces she can't take it anymore, then breaks down along with <a href="/wiki/Finnick" class="mw-redirect" title="Finnick">Finnick</a>. <a href="/wiki/President_Coin" class="mw-redirect" title="President Coin">President Coin</a> assembles a rescue team to retrieve all victors in the Capitol, and Gale is the first person to volunteer.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_11_67-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_11-67"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>67<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> In a daring rescue, <a href="/wiki/Peeta" class="mw-redirect" title="Peeta">Peeta</a> and other victors, apart from Enobaria, are rescued from the Capitol. Katniss feels helpless when she is left out of the rescue mission. When she goes to visit Finnick, the two talk about the mission and how if they die, it won't be bad because they won't have to feel any more pain. Haymitch informs them that they can help by shooting a propaganda. Katniss does an interview and tells them how she met Peeta and informs them of the time when President Snow told her the Capitol is fragile. She tells them it is because the Capitol depends on the districts, and without them, they would fall. Katniss is informed the rescue party had arrived home safe. She sees Johanna and Annie are alive and that Peeta has been saved.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_12_68-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_12-68"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>68<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> However, Peeta has been <a href="/wiki/Hijacking" title="Hijacking">hijacked</a> into hating Katniss<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_13_69-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_13-69"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>69<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, and tries to strangle her to death upon their reunion<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_12_68-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_12-68"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>68<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>.
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Hijacked_Peeta">Hijacked Peeta</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D41&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>Katniss receives medical attention after the attempted strangulation. Prim comforts Katniss and they are informed by Plutarch that Peeta was brainwashed by the Capitol and is receiving all the treatment they can give him. Katniss is deeply depressed by this and goes to visit Gale and Beetee. She is sad that Gale and Beetee are building weapons to help their efforts but aren't thinking about the innocent people who may be injured in the process. Katniss meets Delly Cartwright and informs her of Peeta's condition. Katniss watches Delly talk to Peeta and his outburst to her, shouting that Katniss is a muttation and District 12's destruction is her fault. Katniss tells Plutarch if she is going to be the mockingjay, he must send her away from Peeta. She asks to be taken to the Capitol so she can end the war by killing Snow, but Plutarch tells her she can't until all the districts are on the rebels' side.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_13_69-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_13-69"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>69<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Going_to_District_2">Going to District 2</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D42&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>Plutarch tells Katniss District 2 is the only district still with the Capitol because District 2 and the Capitol have a close relationship with each other. District 2 supplies the Capitol with <a href="/wiki/Peacekeepers" title="Peacekeepers">Peacekeepers</a> and Stone, and the Capitol pays a lot in return, therefore being a wealthier district<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_14_70-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_14-70"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>70<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, and so she asks to be sent to District 2<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_13_69-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_13-69"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>69<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>.
</p><p>Katniss goes to <a href="/wiki/District_2" title="District 2">District 2</a> with Haymitch, Boggs, Gale<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_14_70-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_14-70"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>70<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, and Beetee and meets <a href="/wiki/Lyme" title="Lyme">Lyme</a> at The Nut<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_14_70-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_14-70"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>70<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. While there, the group tries to think of a way to attack in, which would give them control over District 2. Gale suggests that they blow up the inside of The Nut<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_14_70-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_14-70"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>70<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, which upsets Katniss because Gale is willing to kill innocent people for the rebellion. In the end, and in the absence of Katniss and Gale having gone hunting, they adopt the solution proposed by Boggs of collapsing the mountain, without touching the train tunnel, to thus allow the evacuation of the surviving citizens of District 2. Witness to the attack on the Nut by Gale's bombs from the roof of the Hall of Justice, Katniss remembers the day of her father's death, and only the voice of Haymitch returning to District 13 through her earpiece brings her back to reality, before ordering her to go inside to avoid possible reprisals from the Capitol by air.
</p><p>Arriving downstairs, she sits down at the foot of one of the immense columns in the entrance hall and, seeing behind the door the marble floor which leads to the steps towards the main square, remembers the feeling of unease she had experienced during the Victory Tour. Joining her, Boggs tells her the decision they have made regarding the attack on the Nut and, feeling that she is freezing, goes to get her a blanket. Meanwhile, Haymitch contacts her to let her know of a small breakthrough regarding Peeta's condition.
</p><p>The fight between the rebels and the peacemakers and other enemies of District 2 lasts into the night. After midnight, Cressida comes to put a microphone on Katniss' Mockingjay costume and Haymitch asks her to give a speech which he promises to supervise. He asks her to announce to everyone that the Nut is defeated, that the Capitol no longer has any control over District 2, thinking that she will perhaps succeed in convincing the rest of their adversaries to lay down their arms.
</p><p>Although initially at odds with him, she eventually capitulates, although she has to hurry, as she is too exposed. Her microphone on, she begins her speech to the citizens of District 2. Despite the fight continuing, Katniss continues her speech until she sees a young man stumbling out of the station. The young man being burned, she rushes towards him, preventing the rebels from attacking him. Once standing, the young man aims his gun at her, but Katniss, who instinctively backed away, makes him understand that she means him no harm. When he asks her to give him just one reason not to kill her, Katniss, confused, doesn't know what to say, leaving him undecided and perplexed. Desperate, she ends up reminding him of the recent events concerning them and declares that she no longer wants to kill the slaves of the Capitol for him, as she had to do during her first Games. She adds that although they pass the buck, it is not the Districts who will win in the end, but the Capitol for whom she is tired of being their pawn, echoing the discussion had with Peeta the day before of the 74th Hunger Games. Encouraged by Haymitch, she continues by reminding them who the real enemy is. While many seem convinced by this inspiring speech, someone in the crowd ends up shooting her.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_15_71-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_15-71"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>71<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Battle_of_the_Capitol">Battle of the Capitol</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D43&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<figure class="thumb  show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 180px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/6/69/Gale_katnissatcapitol.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20140531122307" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss and  dressed as Capitol citizens." src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="180" height="148" class="thumbimage lazyload" data-image-name="Gale katnissatcapitol.jpg" data-image-key="Gale_katnissatcapitol.jpg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/6/69/Gale_katnissatcapitol.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20140531122307"></a> 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/6/69/Gale_katnissatcapitol.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20140531122307" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss and  dressed as Capitol citizens." src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/6/69/Gale_katnissatcapitol.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20140531122307" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="180" height="148" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="Gale katnissatcapitol.jpg" data-image-key="Gale_katnissatcapitol.jpg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/6/69/Gale_katnissatcapitol.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/180?cb=20140531122307"></a></noscript> 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:Gale_katnissatcapitol.jpg" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Katniss and <a href="/wiki/Gale_Hawthorne" title="Gale Hawthorne">Gale</a> dressed as Capitol citizens.</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<p>The rebels take control of the districts<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_16_72-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_16-72"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>72<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> and finally begin an assault on the Capitol itself, which Katniss is a part of. However, an assault on a "safe" Capitol neighborhood goes wrong, and Katniss and her team flee further into the Capitol with the intent of finding and killing President Snow. While Peeta is hijacked, he is driven to kill Katniss but fought it off by remembering memories of Katniss and his deep love for her. Peeta wanted to die because he had impulses to kill her; on several occasions, he asks her to shoot him, give him nightlock, or just leave him to die. Peeta feared he would eventually overpower Katniss. Nonetheless, she stayed by him and comforted him. Eventually, they both became closer, and their love became deeper. The squad goes further into the Capitol, dressed as Capitol citizens. Eventually, Katniss finds herself pressing on alone towards Snow's mansion which has supposedly been opened to shelter Capitol children (but is actually intended to provide human shields for Snow). Afterward, bombs placed in supply packages kill many of these children and a rebel medical team, including Katniss' sister, Prim.
</p><p>Katniss is burned by the explosion but receives medical attention at the Capitol hospital. <a href="/wiki/President_Coin" class="mw-redirect" title="President Coin">President Coin</a> visits her and tells her the rebels have won and she has saved Snow for Katniss to kill. Though Snow is tried and found guilty, he tells Katniss the final assault that killed Prim was ordered by <a href="/wiki/President_Coin" class="mw-redirect" title="President Coin">President Coin</a>, not the Capitol. Katniss realizes that if that was true, the bombing may have been the result of a plan originally developed by <a href="/wiki/Gale" class="mw-redirect" title="Gale">Gale</a>. Katniss doubts this, but still realizes that she will never be able to look at Gale the same way, regardless of whether he was directly involved in Prim's death.
</p>
<h4><span class="mw-headline" id="Death_of_President_Alma_Coin">Death of President Alma Coin</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D44&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h4>
<p>Coin gathers the seven remaining victors who survived and proposes a final Hunger Games but with the children of the most powerful people in the Capitol. <a href="/wiki/Peeta" class="mw-redirect" title="Peeta">Peeta</a>, <a href="/wiki/Annie" class="mw-redirect" title="Annie">Annie</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Beetee" class="mw-redirect" title="Beetee">Beetee</a> vote no, but <a href="/wiki/Johanna" class="mw-redirect" title="Johanna">Johanna</a>, <a href="/wiki/Enobaria" title="Enobaria">Enobaria</a>, <a href="/wiki/Haymitch" class="mw-redirect" title="Haymitch">Haymitch</a>, and Katniss all vote yes.
</p>
<figure class="thumb mw-halign-left show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 317px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/d/d0/Screen_Shot_2015-06-09_at_2.47.53_PM.png/revision/latest?cb=20150609222204" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss about to kill ." src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="317" height="133" class="thumbimage lazyload" data-image-name="Screen Shot 2015-06-09 at 2.47.53 PM.png" data-image-key="Screen_Shot_2015-06-09_at_2.47.53_PM.png" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/d/d0/Screen_Shot_2015-06-09_at_2.47.53_PM.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/317?cb=20150609222204"></a> 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/d/d0/Screen_Shot_2015-06-09_at_2.47.53_PM.png/revision/latest?cb=20150609222204" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss about to kill ." src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/d/d0/Screen_Shot_2015-06-09_at_2.47.53_PM.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/317?cb=20150609222204" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="317" height="133" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="Screen Shot 2015-06-09 at 2.47.53 PM.png" data-image-key="Screen_Shot_2015-06-09_at_2.47.53_PM.png" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/d/d0/Screen_Shot_2015-06-09_at_2.47.53_PM.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/317?cb=20150609222204"></a></noscript> 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:Screen_Shot_2015-06-09_at_2.47.53_PM.png" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Katniss about to kill <a href="/wiki/Coriolanus_Snow" title="Coriolanus Snow">Snow</a>.</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<p>Katniss remembers a conversation with Snow in which they promised not to lie to each other. When she is supposed to execute Snow, she realizes that he was telling the truth and kills Coin instead. A riot ensues and Snow is found mysteriously dead, having possibly choked on his own blood or trampled by the crowd. Katniss is admitted to a secure holding room but acquitted due to her apparent insanity and returns to her home in <a href="/wiki/District_12" title="District 12">District 12</a>, while Gale goes to District 2 to work in the military and Mrs. Everdeen begins practicing at a District 4 hospital, unable to go back to District 12 due to the pain. Still destroyed over the death of Prim, Katniss becomes depressed and suicidal.
</p><p>Peeta returns soon after as well, having recovered immensely from his brainwashing. He plants primroses around Katniss' house as a memorial for her sister. Katniss begins to heal, and her relationships with Peeta and Haymitch recover as well. After a while, Katniss and Peeta put together a book of all of the characters that died at the end of the series. Katniss writes all of the facts about the victims, including Rue, Prim, Boggs, Finnick, and more. Peeta draws all of the pictures in the book, if they had not found a picture of them.
</p><p>Peeta plays one final round of Real or Not and says, "you love me, real or not real?". Katniss answers back, "real".
</p>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Adult_life">Adult life</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D45&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<p>In the epilogue, Katniss explains that she agreed to have children with Peeta fifteen years after the war, when she was about 32-33. The epilogue itself is set "more than twenty years" after the war. In those five or more intervening years, she and Peeta have had two children, both unnamed: a <a href="/wiki/Katniss_and_Peeta_Mellark%27s_daughter" title="Katniss and Peeta Mellark's daughter">daughter</a> and a <a href="/wiki/Katniss_and_Peeta_Mellark%27s_son" title="Katniss and Peeta Mellark's son">son</a>. The Hunger Games are over, the arenas have been destroyed, and memorials have been built for the tributes and others, who died in the wars. She and Peeta have settled in the <a href="/wiki/The_Meadow" title="The Meadow">Meadow</a>, where their children can play in peace and harmony. She dreads the day her children's innocent world is shattered by learning the details of their parents' involvement in both the Games and the war, though Peeta assures her that they will be okay, that they have each other and the memory book. Katniss is still affected by <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/post-traumatic_stress_disorder" class="extiw" title="wikipedia:post-traumatic stress disorder">post-traumatic stress disorder</a> in the years following the Games and the war. When she feels upset, she has taken to reminding herself of every good thing that she has ever seen someone do. She ruefully acknowledges that this routine is a bit of a tedious game for her, but that "there are much worse games to play".
</p><p>Katniss and Peeta eventually create a memorial book. While Haymitch wants nothing to do with it at first, he eventually shares with them his own story about all of the people that Haymitch had loved and lost. Afterwards, Katniss brings Haymitch some goose eggs and Peeta builds an incubator for them.<sup id="cite_ref-SOTRE_73-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-SOTRE-73"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>73<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Relationships">Relationships</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D46&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Katniss_Everdeen/Relationships" title="Katniss Everdeen/Relationships">Katniss Everdeen Relationships</a></li></ul>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Victims">Victims</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D47&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<p>During the <a href="/wiki/74th_Hunger_Games" title="74th Hunger Games">74th Hunger Games</a>, Katniss killed <a href="/wiki/Glimmer" title="Glimmer">Glimmer</a>, the female tribute from <a href="/wiki/District_1" title="District 1">District 1</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Tippet" title="Tippet">Tippet</a> by dropping a tracker jacker hive on them while they slept. The two received many stings, which resulted in death.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_14_27-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_14-27"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>27<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Her next kill was <a href="/wiki/Marvel" title="Marvel">Marvel</a>, the male tribute from <a href="/wiki/District_1" title="District 1">District 1</a>, who drowned in his own blood after Katniss shot him in the neck after he threw a spear into Rue's stomach<sup id="cite_ref-THG_18_33-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_18-33"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>33<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. During the final fight between Katniss, <a href="/wiki/Peeta" class="mw-redirect" title="Peeta">Peeta</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Cato" title="Cato">Cato</a> (the male tribute from <a href="/wiki/District_2" title="District 2">District 2</a>) <a href="/wiki/Cato" title="Cato">Cato</a> was mortally wounded by muttations. Katniss killed him by shooting an arrow at his neck, as an act of mercy.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_25_3-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_25-3"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>During the 75th Hunger Games, Katniss killed Gloss, the male tribute from District 1, after he slit Wiress's throat<sup id="cite_ref-CF_23_57-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_23-57"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>57<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>.
</p><p>After joining the rebellion, Katniss and Gale shoot down several Capitol bombers during an air raid on District 8<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_7_64-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_7-64"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>64<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. During the <a href="/wiki/Battle_of_the_Capitol" title="Battle of the Capitol">Battle of the Capitol</a>, she kills <a href="/wiki/Peacekeepers" title="Peacekeepers">Peacekeepers</a>, a female Capitol citizen, and at Snow's execution, <a href="/wiki/President_Coin" class="mw-redirect" title="President Coin">President Coin</a>.
</p>
