<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Physical_description">Physical description</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D48&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<figure class="thumb mw-halign-left show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 200px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/2/2e/Screen_Shot_2012-02-08_at_7.48.07_PM.png/revision/latest?cb=20120209005629" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss in her Reaping clothes." src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="200" height="264" class="thumbimage lazyload" data-image-name="Screen Shot 2012-02-08 at 7.48.07 PM.png" data-image-key="Screen_Shot_2012-02-08_at_7.48.07_PM.png" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/2/2e/Screen_Shot_2012-02-08_at_7.48.07_PM.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/200?cb=20120209005629"></a> 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/2/2e/Screen_Shot_2012-02-08_at_7.48.07_PM.png/revision/latest?cb=20120209005629" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss in her Reaping clothes." src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/2/2e/Screen_Shot_2012-02-08_at_7.48.07_PM.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/200?cb=20120209005629" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="200" height="264" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="Screen Shot 2012-02-08 at 7.48.07 PM.png" data-image-key="Screen_Shot_2012-02-08_at_7.48.07_PM.png" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/2/2e/Screen_Shot_2012-02-08_at_7.48.07_PM.png/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/200?cb=20120209005629"></a></noscript> 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:Screen_Shot_2012-02-08_at_7.48.07_PM.png" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Katniss in her Reaping clothes.</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<p>Katniss has long black hair, which she normally pulls back into a long braid, olive skin, and gray eyes<sup id="cite_ref-THG_1_2-8" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_1-2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. After the fire in the 74th Hunger Games, after her time in the arena, her hair turned at least six inches short<sup id="cite_ref-THG_13_26-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_13-26"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>26<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, it is an unknown if it was grown back - though most likely it was after her full body fix in the Capitol. She is small in stature and thin for her age as she was generally malnourished because of her district's poverty. She is thus one of the smallest tributes in her Games, but due to her outdoor life, she is strong for her size.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_7_20-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_7-20"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>20<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> She is also known as "The Girl on Fire" during the Hunger Games; she earns this title from her fierce personality and the outfits created for her by her stylist, <a href="/wiki/Cinna" title="Cinna">Cinna</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_5_18-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_5-18"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>18<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Katniss is not very image-conscious, to the dismay of <a href="/wiki/Venia" title="Venia">Venia</a>, one of her stylists<sup id="cite_ref-THG_5_18-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_5-18"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>18<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_3_13-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_3-13"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_12_4-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_12-4"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. In the first book, her hearing in her left ear is destroyed<sup id="cite_ref-THG_17_29-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_17-29"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>29<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> because of the explosion caused in the arena<sup id="cite_ref-THG_16_28-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_16-28"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>28<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, but it is fixed in the Capitol in the end, as are all of her other major and more minor injuries including the erasing of any scars from her years of hunting<sup id="cite_ref-THG_26_30-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_26-30"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>30<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. Before entering the arena the first time, Katniss had many minor scrapes and cuts, and her outward appearance was very much imperfect. However, after she won, they were repaired by the Capitol doctors, including replacing the hearing in her left ear<sup id="cite_ref-THG_26_30-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_26-30"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>30<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. Most notable about her appearance and demeanor, however, was the image she unconsciously projected, as noted by her co-tribute <a href="/wiki/Peeta" class="mw-redirect" title="Peeta">Peeta</a><sup id="cite_ref-THG_7_20-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_7-20"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>20<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. In Peeta's interview, he says that a lot of boys like Katniss when asked if he likes anyone at home<sup id="cite_ref-THG_9_21-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_9-21"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>21<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_10_22-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_10-22"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>.
</p><p>In <i>The Hunger Games</i>, Katniss wore worn-out clothes with hunting boots<sup id="cite_ref-THG_1_2-9" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_1-2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, and sometimes wore her father's old hunting coat<sup id="cite_ref-THG_2_11-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_2-11"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. During the reaping, she wore her mother's blue dress<sup id="cite_ref-THG_1_2-10" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_1-2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. At the parade in the capitol, she wore the costume that Cinna made that gave her the name "The Girl on Fire." She wore a black unitard representing coal and lit them with synthetic fire that doesn't burn but can be quenched by a solution made to counter it.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_5_18-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_5-18"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>18<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> He keeps the theme by designing Katniss's interview dress<sup id="cite_ref-THG_8_16-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_8-16"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, a gown made completely of precious jewels in flame colors, reflecting the lighting to make her look like she is "engulfed in flames." <sup id="cite_ref-THG_9_21-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_9-21"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>21<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> During the Hunger Games, she wore a hooded black jacket that fell down to her thighs and reflects body heat, tawny trousers, a sturdy brown belt, and a green button-up shirt. She also wears her Mockingjay pin.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_10_22-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_10-22"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Cinna" title="Cinna">Cinna</a> also designs her Victor dress, keeping the theme of fire, yet toning it down to candlelight to symbolize innocence other than the fiery spirit for which the theme was first created<sup id="cite_ref-THG_26_30-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_26-30"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>30<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_27_32-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_27-32"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>32<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>.
</p>
<figure class="thumb  show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 226px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/c/c2/Catching_fire_promo_katniss.jpeg/revision/latest?cb=20130306023810" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss Everdeen's Capitol portrait." src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="226" height="334" class="thumbimage lazyload" data-image-name="Catching fire promo katniss.jpeg" data-image-key="Catching_fire_promo_katniss.jpeg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/c/c2/Catching_fire_promo_katniss.jpeg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/226?cb=20130306023810"></a> 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/c/c2/Catching_fire_promo_katniss.jpeg/revision/latest?cb=20130306023810" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Katniss Everdeen's Capitol portrait." src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/c/c2/Catching_fire_promo_katniss.jpeg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/226?cb=20130306023810" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="226" height="334" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="Catching fire promo katniss.jpeg" data-image-key="Catching_fire_promo_katniss.jpeg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/c/c2/Catching_fire_promo_katniss.jpeg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/226?cb=20130306023810"></a></noscript> 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:Catching_fire_promo_katniss.jpeg" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Katniss Everdeen's Capitol portrait.</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure>
<p>In <i>Catching Fire</i>, she wore many dresses due to the victory tour held six months after the games are over. Katniss describes the outfit Cinna designs for the first day of the Victory Tour as having "flowing black pants made of a thick, warm material. A comfortable white shirt. A sweater woven from green and blue and gray strands of kitten-soft wool. Laced leather boots that don't pinch my toes." Another one of Cinna's outfits for the Victory Tour includes a white coat, leather gloves, a bright red scarf, and earmuffs.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_3_13-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_3-13"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> The outfit Katniss wears in District 11 is described as "a pretty orange frock patterned with autumn leaves."<sup id="cite_ref-CF_4_40-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_4-40"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>40<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Cinna" title="Cinna">Cinna</a> designs dresses for Katniss to wear to banquets and balls. One is described by Katniss as pale pink and strapless<sup id="cite_ref-CF_5_31-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_5-31"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>31<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. Another is described by Johanna Mason as a strapless dark blue velvet dress with diamonds that Katniss wore in District 2<sup id="cite_ref-CF_15_49-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_15-49"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>49<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. The dress worn by <a href="/wiki/Annie_Cresta" title="Annie Cresta">Annie Cresta</a> at her wedding was designed by Cinna for Katniss in District 5<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_16_72-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_16-72"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>72<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. After Katniss accepted Peeta's proposal during the <a href="/wiki/Victory_Tour" title="Victory Tour">Victory Tour</a> stop at the Capitol<sup id="cite_ref-CF_5_31-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_5-31"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>31<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, Cinna designed a set of wedding dresses. The Capitol audience voted on the finalists to select the one that would have been used for the wedding. Katniss describes the winner as having sleeves that touch the floor and lots and lots of pearls.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_12_4-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_12-4"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_17_51-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_17-51"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>51<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> During the third Quarter Quell, she wore a fitted blue jumpsuit only 1/2 inch thick made of sheer material, with a zipper in front, a six-inch wide padded belt covered in purple plastic, and nylon shoes with rubber soles<sup id="cite_ref-CF_18_52-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_18-52"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>52<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. Every victor wore a blue jumpsuit, and a belt that was also a flotation device. The belt was filled with a thick, purple liquid.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_19_53-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_19-53"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>53<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Katniss also wore her Mockingjay pin<sup id="cite_ref-CF_18_52-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_18-52"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>52<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>.
</p><p>In <i>Mockingjay</i>, she wore a plain grey shirt and pants since all residents of <a href="/wiki/District_13" title="District 13">District 13</a> wear this<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_3_1-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_3-1"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. <a href="/wiki/Cinna" title="Cinna">Cinna</a> had designed a uniform for Katniss to wear for her role as the mockingjay. The uniform was black body armor. It had a helmet that can turn into a hood, a chest plate to protect vital organs and had special reinforcements over her heart, short sleeves that reach to her elbows, and white folds under her arms. She had hidden weapons in her boots and belt.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_3_1-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_3-1"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Katniss wears a bloody bandage to cover the scar on her left arm<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_5_62-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_5-62"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>62<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> that <a href="/wiki/Johanna_Mason" title="Johanna Mason">Johanna Mason</a> gave her when she cut her tracker out during the <a href="/wiki/75th_Hunger_Games" title="75th Hunger Games">75th Hunger Games</a><sup id="cite_ref-CF_26_60-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_26-60"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>60<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. She also wears her Mockingjay pin over her heart<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_5_62-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_5-62"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>62<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. It's also said in the book after the explosion in Capitol where her sister died, that some of her hair was burned off. Her Mockingjay suit is red, symbolizing the rebellion and the revolution.
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Personality">Personality</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D49&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<div class="quote">
<dl><dd><span title="Source: Mockingjay">"<i>Frankly, I could need some sugarcoating.</i>"</span></dd>
<dd>—Katniss<sup class="noprint"><a href="/wiki/Mockingjay" title="Mockingjay">[src]</a></sup></dd></dl></div><p> Katniss has strong and independent survivalist instincts due to her difficult past and is good at thinking outside the box. She is not socially adept and has a hard time making friends<sup id="cite_ref-THG_1_2-11" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_1-2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> due to the emotional strain on her life which has made her hard and cold. She is more concerned about feeding her family than being social, making her awkward around people. Though Katniss can be rather blunt, misanthropic, sarcastic, bitingly cynical, and cold, she has a very vulnerable side which she only shows around her family<sup id="cite_ref-CF_11_47-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_11-47"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>47<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_13_48-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_13-48"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>48<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-MJ_3_1-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_3-1"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, and she has a natural maternal instinct as showed when she comforted Prim<sup id="cite_ref-THG_1_2-12" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_1-2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_17-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG-17"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>17<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> and helped Rue<sup id="cite_ref-THG_14_27-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_14-27"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>27<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_15_24-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_15-24"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>24<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_16_28-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_16-28"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>28<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_17_29-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_17-29"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>29<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_18_33-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_18-33"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>33<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. She is not very good at acting for the camera<sup id="cite_ref-THG_10_22-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_10-22"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-MJ_5_62-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_5-62"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>62<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-MJ_15_71-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_15-71"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>71<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> and comes across best when she is herself<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_6_63-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_6-63"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>63<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. She is usually very logical except for times when her emotions get in the way<sup id="cite_ref-THG_2_11-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_2-11"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_7_20-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_7-20"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>20<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_10_22-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_10-22"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_13_26-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_13-26"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>26<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_20_54-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_20-54"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>54<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_23_57-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_23-57"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>57<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_27_7-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_27-7"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-MJ_11_67-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_11-67"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>67<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>.
</p><p>Katniss has a habit of biting her nails when she is nervous or anxious<sup id="cite_ref-THG_7_20-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_7-20"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>20<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_10_22-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_10-22"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_17_29-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_17-29"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>29<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_3_13-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_3-13"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> and must consciously stop herself from doing so when around others<sup id="cite_ref-THG_7_20-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_7-20"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>20<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. After she became a victor she could not think of a good reason to break the habit<sup id="cite_ref-CF_3_13-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_3-13"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. However, after joining the rebellion she appears to have stopped biting her nails as she does not mention doing so.
</p>
<div class="quote">
<dl><dd><span title="Source: Catching Fire">"<i>All I know about you is that you're stubborn and good with a bow."<br>"That pretty much sums me up.</i>"</span></dd>
<dd>—Peeta and Katniss on the train to District 11.<sup class="noprint"><a href="/wiki/Catching_Fire" title="Catching Fire">[src]</a></sup></dd></dl></div>
<p>Katniss is very strong, stubborn, and determined. Often, she will react first and ask questions later<sup id="cite_ref-THG_10_22-7" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_10-22"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. She will do anything to survive<sup id="cite_ref-THG_11_23-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_11-23"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>23<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_12_25-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_12-25"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_13_26-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_13-26"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>26<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_14_27-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_14-27"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>27<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. Even when she allied with <a href="/wiki/Rue" title="Rue">Rue</a>, she said she hoped that she didn't have to be the one to kill her<sup id="cite_ref-THG_15_24-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_15-24"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>24<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_16_28-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_16-28"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>28<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. Yet when it came to <a href="/wiki/Peeta" class="mw-redirect" title="Peeta">Peeta</a>, she tried her best to figure a way for them both to survive<sup id="cite_ref-THG_19_34-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_19-34"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>34<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_20_35-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_20-35"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>35<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_21_36-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_21-36"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>36<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_22_10-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_22-10"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>10<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_23_37-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_23-37"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>37<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_24_38-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_24-38"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>38<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. This independent streak did not go well in the regimented confines of <a href="/wiki/District_13" title="District 13">District 13</a><sup id="cite_ref-MJ_3_1-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_3-1"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-MJ_4_61-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_4-61"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>61<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-MJ_17_74-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_17-74"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>74<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> and Katniss largely did what she wanted and ignored her daily schedule<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_2_6-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_2-6"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-MJ_3_1-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_3-1"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-MJ_17_74-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_17-74"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>74<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. She had to discipline herself to undergo military training in order to prove that she could be sent into combat<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_17_74-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_17-74"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>74<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, and her unwillingness to take orders was perceived as her greatest weakness. This was targeted during her individual exam, but she was able to realize this and pass.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_18_75-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_18-75"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>75<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>Even though she can sometimes be a bit self-centered and self-reliant, she does have noble traits, protecting those she loves<sup id="cite_ref-THG_1_2-13" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_1-2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_2_11-7" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_2-11"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_14_27-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_14-27"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>27<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_21_36-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_21-36"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>36<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_3_13-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_3-13"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_8_44-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_8-44"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>44<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-MJ_3_1-7" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_3-1"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. For example, caring for Prim<sup id="cite_ref-THG_1_2-14" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_1-2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_2_11-8" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_2-11"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_12_25-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_12-25"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_20_35-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_20-35"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>35<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, looking out for Rue<sup id="cite_ref-THG_14_27-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_14-27"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>27<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_15_24-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_15-24"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>24<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_16_28-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_16-28"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>28<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_17_29-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_17-29"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>29<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_18_33-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_18-33"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>33<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, and ending Cato's life mercifully even though he caused her so much pain<sup id="cite_ref-THG_25_3-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_25-3"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> or when she shields Gale to keep him from being whipped, even when it means a lash for herself<sup id="cite_ref-CF_8_44-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_8-44"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>44<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. Katniss has an instinct to protect those who are weak<sup id="cite_ref-CF_16_50-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_16-50"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>50<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_22_56-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_22-56"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>56<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> such as Rue<sup id="cite_ref-THG_14_27-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_14-27"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>27<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, Wiress<sup id="cite_ref-CF_16_50-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_16-50"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>50<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_23_57-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_23-57"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>57<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, and her prep team<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_5_62-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_5-62"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>62<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>.
</p><p>Unlike her younger sister, she is not a natural born healer<sup id="cite_ref-THG_13_26-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_13-26"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>26<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_18_33-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_18-33"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>33<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. She once ran from the house in fright while Prim and her mother cared for a dying man<sup id="cite_ref-THG_13_26-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_13-26"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>26<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. She even had trouble healing Peeta, being squeamish, and reluctant to care for him unclothed<sup id="cite_ref-THG_19_34-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_19-34"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>34<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. Instead, she is more like her father, being skilled at hunting and gathering.
</p><p>Similar to her father, Katniss had a rebellious streak. She was a fiercely determined and resourceful fighter and was reasonably easy to underestimate<sup id="cite_ref-THG_11_23-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_11-23"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>23<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. Due to this, she was almost always in disagreement with authority figures<sup id="cite_ref-THG_18_33-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_18-33"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>33<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_9_45-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_9-45"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>45<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-MJ_3_1-8" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_3-1"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-MJ_7_64-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_7-64"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>64<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> and liked to use her own resources when it came to survival (the instincts of a true survivor)<sup id="cite_ref-THG_4_12-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_4-12"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_11_23-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_11-23"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>23<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_12_25-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_12-25"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. Katniss was loyal to no one and had issues when it came to trusting others<sup id="cite_ref-THG_4_12-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_4-12"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_7_20-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_7-20"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>20<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_8_16-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_8-16"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_9_21-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_9-21"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>21<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_19_53-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_19-53"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>53<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_20_54-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_20-54"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>54<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_23_57-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_23-57"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>57<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_25_59-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_25-59"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>59<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_26_60-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_26-60"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>60<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_27_7-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_27-7"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-MJ_3_1-9" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_3-1"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-MJ_16_72-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_16-72"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>72<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, but this grew to be her one of her greatest strengths.
</p><p>In <i>Mockingjay</i>, it is shown that Katniss dislikes being a "pawn" and hates having the weight of Panem on her shoulders<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_5_62-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_5-62"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>62<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-MJ_15_71-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_15-71"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>71<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>.
</p><p>Katniss suffers from PTSD from her experiences in the Hunger Games throughout the series. This includes frequent nightmares and night terrors of those she killed and tried to kill her, avoidance of others/isolation, irritability, and favoring the wilds of the forest over human contact.<sup id="cite_ref-76" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-76"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>76<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Family">Family</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D50&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<p>At the start of the trilogy, Katniss's only family members are her sister Prim and their mother. Her father died in a mine explosion when she was eleven<sup id="cite_ref-THG_1_2-15" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_1-2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. Katniss would do anything to protect Prim,<sup id="cite_ref-THG_4_12-7" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_4-12"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, even if it means her own death<sup id="cite_ref-THG_2_11-9" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_2-11"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. Her mother suffered extreme depression after the death of her husband, leaving Katniss to become the head of the family, despite her young age<sup id="cite_ref-THG_4_12-8" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_4-12"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. Mrs. Everdeen is a skilled healer, and Prim is something of an assistant<sup id="cite_ref-THG_13_26-7" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_13-26"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>26<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_8_44-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_8-44"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>44<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>.
</p><p><a href="/wiki/Gale_Hawthorne" title="Gale Hawthorne">Gale Hawthorne</a> and his family are like family for her<sup id="cite_ref-THG_1_2-16" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_1-2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. In <i>Catching Fire</i>, it is revealed that during the Hunger Games, Gale was presented to the <a href="/wiki/Capitol" class="mw-redirect" title="Capitol">Capitol</a> media as Katniss' cousin, in order to be interviewed about her without detracting from the romance which Katniss and Peeta were presenting to survive the arena<sup id="cite_ref-CF_1_8-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_1-8"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>8<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. After the 74th Hunger Games conclude, President Snow threatens Katniss with Gale's life if she does not perpetuate this lie<sup id="cite_ref-CF_2_39-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_2-39"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>39<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, and so she does<sup id="cite_ref-CF_4_40-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_4-40"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>40<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. As part of this sham romance, Peeta and Katniss become engaged<sup id="cite_ref-CF_5_31-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_5-31"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>31<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, and Peeta tells a TV interviewer that Katniss is pregnant<sup id="cite_ref-CF_18_52-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_18-52"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>52<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. It is revealed early in <i>Catching Fire</i> that Gale loves Katniss<sup id="cite_ref-CF_7_43-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_7-43"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>43<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. Even though her father is dead, she still misses him dearly. With the possible exception of Prim, there was no one Katniss loved more than her father. He taught her to hunt<sup id="cite_ref-THG_4_12-9" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_4-12"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, swim<sup id="cite_ref-CF_3_13-7" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_3-13"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, and live off the wild<sup id="cite_ref-THG_4_12-10" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_4-12"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, skills that served her well during the games. Katniss inherits a beautiful singing voice from her father<sup id="cite_ref-THG_3_14-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_3-14"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_18_33-7" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_18-33"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>33<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_22_10-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_22-10"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>10<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, shown when either of them sing the mockingjays stop to listen<sup id="cite_ref-THG_3_14-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_3-14"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_22_10-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_22-10"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>10<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. He taught her many songs such as "The Hanging Tree"<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_9_15-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_9-15"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> and the lullaby Katniss sang to Rue as she died<sup id="cite_ref-THG_18_33-8" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_18-33"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>33<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. But after her father's death, singing reminds her too much of her father so she only does it rarely<sup id="cite_ref-THG_3_14-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_3-14"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_18_33-9" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_18-33"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>33<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>.
</p><p>When Katniss's mom heard Katniss singing "The Hanging Tree" she yelled at him for teaching her that, and tells Katniss to forget the song. Katniss runs away to the hideout and of course, because she was told to forget the song, she distinctly remembers it.<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_9_15-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_9-15"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> She remembers it a lot, especially after her father's death. Katniss sings the "Hanging Tree" to <a href="/wiki/Pollux" title="Pollux">Pollux</a> when he wanted her to sing for the mockingjays<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_9_15-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_9-15"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> and a few other times throughout the book <i>Mockingjay</i><sup id="cite_ref-MJ_12_68-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_12-68"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>68<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. Katniss sings <a href="/wiki/Songs#%22Deep_in_the_Meadow%22" title="Songs">"Deep in the Meadow"</a> to her two children, and many times throughout the series<sup id="cite_ref-THG_18_33-10" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_18-33"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>33<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_17-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG-17"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>17<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>.
</p><p>The rebellion drives Katniss's family apart. Prim's death in a bombing devastates Katniss. The trauma leaves her a "mental Avox"<sup id="cite_ref-MJ_25_77-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_25-77"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>77<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, unable to speak until her encounter with Snow after his imprisonment. Her mother leaves District 12 permanently, taking a position in District 4 to build a hospital. Katniss returns home with Haymitch, where she finds that Peeta has also returned, yet Gale has not. Katniss and Peeta rely on each other while recuperating from the war, and rebuild their relationship.
</p><p>Fifteen years after the war, Katniss and Peeta have a daughter with black hair like her mother, and blue eyes like her father. Their second child is a boy with his father's blond hair and his mother's gray eyes.
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Etymology">Etymology</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D51&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<div class="quote">
<dl><dd><span title="Source: https://www.nytimes.com/2018/10/18/books/suzanne-collins-talks-about-the-hunger-games-the-books-and-the-movies.html">"<i>[Katniss Everdeen's] name came later, while I was researching survival training and specifically edible plants. In one of my books, I found the arrowhead plant, and the more I read about it, the more it seemed to reflect her. Its Latin name has the same roots as <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sagittarius_(constellation)" class="extiw" title="wikipedia:Sagittarius (constellation)">Sagittarius</a>, the archer. The edible tuber roots she could gather, the arrowhead-shaped leaves were her defense, and the little white blossoms kept it in the tradition of flower names, like Rue and Primrose. I looked at the list of alternative names for it. Swamp Potato. Duck Potato. Katniss easily won the day.</i>"</span></dd>
<dd>—<a href="/wiki/Suzanne_Collins" title="Suzanne Collins">Suzanne Collins</a> on naming Katniss Everdeen.<sup class="noprint">[<a target="_blank" rel="nofollow noreferrer noopener" class="external text" href="https://www.nytimes.com/2018/10/18/books/suzanne-collins-talks-about-the-hunger-games-the-books-and-the-movies.html%7C">[src</a>]]</sup></dd></dl></div>
<p>The katniss<sup id="cite_ref-THG_1_2-17" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_1-2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> plant is also known as "arrowhead"<sup id="cite_ref-THG_4_12-11" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_4-12"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> and belongs to the genus <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sagittaria" class="extiw" title="wikipedia:Sagittaria">Sagittaria</a>. It is both ornamental and useful, as its tubers are edible.
</p><p>According to Suzanne Collins, Katniss's last name comes from Bathsheba Everdene, the main character in <i><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Far_from_the_Madding_Crowd" class="extiw" title="wikipedia:Far from the Madding Crowd">Far from the Madding Crowd</a></i>, because "both struggle with knowing their hearts".<sup id="cite_ref-78" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-78"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>78<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>
</p><p>She was given two other names due to her symbolic roles: the Girl on Fire<sup id="cite_ref-THG_5_18-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_5-18"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>18<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> and the Mockingjay.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_27_7-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_27-7"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> The first was a reference to her spectacular costume during the chariot ride before her first Games<sup id="cite_ref-THG_5_18-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_5-18"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>18<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> and her dresses<sup id="cite_ref-THG_8_16-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_8-16"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_9_21-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_9-21"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>21<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_17_51-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_17-51"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>51<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_18_52-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_18-52"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>52<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>. The second was because of her district token, a gold mockingjay pin<sup id="cite_ref-THG_3_14-7" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_3-14"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup>, which came to stand for the rebellion.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_6_42-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_6-42"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>42<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_10_46-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_10-46"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>46<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_14_79-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_14-79"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>79<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-CF_27_7-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_27-7"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Both names originated from something she wore.
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Trivia">Trivia</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D52&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<figure class="thumb  show-info-icon" typeof="mw:File/Thumb" style="width: 210px"> 	<a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/7/7d/Jennifer_Lawrence_on_Hunger_Games_set.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20120425185911" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Jennifer Lawrence on the set of The Hunger Games." src="data:image/gif;base64,R0lGODlhAQABAIABAAAAAP///yH5BAEAAAEALAAAAAABAAEAQAICTAEAOw%3D%3D" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="210" height="140" class="thumbimage lazyload" data-image-name="Jennifer Lawrence on Hunger Games set.jpg" data-image-key="Jennifer_Lawrence_on_Hunger_Games_set.jpg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/7/7d/Jennifer_Lawrence_on_Hunger_Games_set.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/210?cb=20120425185911"></a> 	<noscript><a href="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/7/7d/Jennifer_Lawrence_on_Hunger_Games_set.jpg/revision/latest?cb=20120425185911" class="mw-file-description image"><img alt="Jennifer Lawrence on the set of The Hunger Games." src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/7/7d/Jennifer_Lawrence_on_Hunger_Games_set.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/210?cb=20120425185911" decoding="async" loading="lazy" width="210" height="140" class="thumbimage" data-image-name="Jennifer Lawrence on Hunger Games set.jpg" data-image-key="Jennifer_Lawrence_on_Hunger_Games_set.jpg" data-relevant="1" data-src="https://static.wikia.nocookie.net/thehungergames/images/7/7d/Jennifer_Lawrence_on_Hunger_Games_set.jpg/revision/latest/scale-to-width-down/210?cb=20120425185911"></a></noscript> 	<figcaption class="thumbcaption"> 		 			<a href="/wiki/File:Jennifer_Lawrence_on_Hunger_Games_set.jpg" class="info-icon"><svg><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-info-small"></use></svg></a> 		 		 		 			<p class="caption">Jennifer Lawrence on the set of <i>The Hunger Games</i>.</p> 		 	</figcaption> </figure> 
<ul><li>Katniss's birthday is May 8th.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_2_11-10" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_2-11"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> Despite thematic associations with <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sagittarius_(astrology)" class="extiw" title="wikipedia:Sagittarius (astrology)">Sagittarius</a> (the archer), Katniss's birthday actually makes her a <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Taurus_(astrology)" class="extiw" title="wikipedia:Taurus (astrology)">Taurus</a>.</li>
<li>Her favorite color is green, and her favorite food in the Capitol is lamb stew with dried plums served on grain.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_4_40-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_4-40"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>40<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>All the tributes she killed in the Games were Career Tributes.
<ul><li>Katniss killed both male <a href="/wiki/Tribute" title="Tribute">tributes</a> from <a href="/wiki/District_1" title="District 1">District 1</a> in her two Games after they killed one of her allies; she killed <a href="/wiki/Marvel" title="Marvel">Marvel</a> after he threw the spear into <a href="/wiki/Rue" title="Rue">Rue's</a> stomach,<sup id="cite_ref-THG_17_29-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_17-29"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>29<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_18_33-11" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_18-33"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>33<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> and she killed <a href="/wiki/Gloss" title="Gloss">Gloss</a> after he slit <a href="/wiki/Wiress" title="Wiress">Wiress's</a> throat.<sup id="cite_ref-CF_23_57-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-CF_23-57"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>57<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul></li>
<li>In the first film, Katniss weighed 122 pounds and had 23-1 <a href="/wiki/Training_score" title="Training score">odds</a> of winning the Games.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_17-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG-17"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>17<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>The two people in her family closest to her, her <a href="/wiki/Mr._Everdeen" class="mw-redirect" title="Mr. Everdeen">father</a> and <a href="/wiki/Primrose_Everdeen" title="Primrose Everdeen">Prim</a>, both died in explosions.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_1_2-18" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_1-2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-80" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-80"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>80<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-MJ_25_77-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_25-77"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>77<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Katniss received burns in all three books.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_13_26-8" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_13-26"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>26<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-81" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-81"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>81<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-MJ_25_77-2" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_25-77"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>77<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Katniss and Peeta are the only tributes to participate in the Games two years in a row.</li>
<li>In the films, she was District 12's very first volunteer according to <a href="/wiki/Effie_Trinket" title="Effie Trinket">Effie</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_17-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG-17"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>17<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> However, in the books, District 12 "hadn't had a volunteer in decades",<sup id="cite_ref-THG_2_11-11" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_2-11"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> suggesting that Katniss was not the first.</li>
<li>Katniss's father made her first bow and arrow.<sup id="cite_ref-THG_1_2-19" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_1-2"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-THG_4_12-12" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-THG_4-12"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Since some of the <a href="/wiki/Covey" title="Covey">Covey</a>'s songs survived into the trilogy through Katniss, who learned them from her father, fans have theorized that the Everdeens are descendants of the Covey— probably through <a href="/wiki/Maude_Ivory_Baird" title="Maude Ivory Baird">Maude Ivory Baird</a>, who shared Katniss's ability to memorize music after a listen or two.<sup id="cite_ref-Ballad_12_82-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Ballad_12-82"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>82<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-MJ_9_15-7" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-MJ_9-15"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> <a href="/wiki/Francis_Lawrence" title="Francis Lawrence">Francis Lawrence</a>, the director of <a href="/wiki/The_Hunger_Games:_The_Ballad_of_Songbirds_and_Snakes" class="mw-redirect" title="The Hunger Games: The Ballad of Songbirds and Snakes"><i>The Ballad of Songbirds and Snakes</i></a> film, supports this theory himself.<sup id="cite_ref-83" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-83"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>83<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul>
<h3><span class="mw-headline" id="Meta">Meta</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D53&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h3>
<ul><li>Katniss's birthday coincides with <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Victory_in_Europe_Day" class="extiw" title="wikipedia:Victory in Europe Day">Victory in Europe Day</a>, celebrating Germany's surrender in WWII. It is also the day that <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joan_of_Arc" class="extiw" title="wikipedia:Joan of Arc">Joan of Arc</a> ended the <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Siege_of_Orl%C3%A9ans" class="extiw" title="wikipedia:Siege of Orléans">Siege of Orléans</a>. However, Suzanne Collins has not indicated whether these connections were intentional or coincidental.
<ul><li>Katniss has often been compared to Joan of Arc. For example, <a href="/wiki/Jennifer_Lawrence" title="Jennifer Lawrence">Jennifer Lawrence</a> described her character as "a futuristic Joan of Arc" while promoting <i>The Hunger Games</i> film,<sup id="cite_ref-84" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-84"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>84<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup> and <a href="/wiki/Donald_Sutherland" title="Donald Sutherland">Donald Sutherland</a> described her as "more dangerous than Joan of Arc" in his initial letter to director <a href="/wiki/Gary_Ross" title="Gary Ross">Gary Ross</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-85" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-85"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>85<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li>
<li>Suzanne Collins has called Katniss a "futuristic <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Theseus" class="extiw" title="wikipedia:Theseus">Theseus</a>".<sup id="cite_ref-86" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-86"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>86<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul></li>
<li>Lionsgate confirmed in March 2011 that about 30 actresses auditioned or read for the role of Katniss, including Hailee Steinfeld, Abigail Breslin, Emma Roberts, Saoirse Ronan, Chloë Moretz, Jodelle Ferland, Lyndsy Fonseca, Emily Browning, Shailene Woodley, Danielle Chuchran, and Kaya Scodelario.</li>
<li>On March 16, 2011, Jennifer Lawrence was announced as the film's Katniss. She was a fan of the books, but took three days to accept the role because she was intimidated by the size of the production.
<ul><li>According to Suzanne Collins, Lawrence was the "only one who truly captured the character I wrote in the book," and that she had "every essential quality necessary to play Katniss."<sup id="cite_ref-87" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-87"><span class="cite-bracket">[</span>87<span class="cite-bracket">]</span></a></sup></li></ul></li>
<li>Katniss's promotional posters for the first three films are unique from other characters'. In a poster for the first film, she's the only character who is looking right; in one for the second, she's the only character standing; and for the third, each character has the film's mockingjay emblem behind them, but Katniss's is aflame.</li></ul>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Gallery">Gallery</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a title="Sign in to edit" class="mw-editsection-visualeditor" data-tracking-label="log-in-edit-section" data-action="edit-section" href="https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fthehungergames.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FKatniss_Everdeen%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D54&amp;uselang=en" data-testid="log-in-edit-section"><svg class="wds-icon wds-icon-tiny"><use xlink:href="#wds-icons-pencil-tiny"></use></svg></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
